================================================================
PATH: server/repositories/auth.repo.ts
================================================================

import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import util from 'node:util';
import { eq, lt, ne } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { db, ensureDatabaseReady } from '../database/index';
import { appAuth, authSessions } from '../database/schema';

const scryptAsync = util.promisify(crypto.scrypt);

const AUTH_SINGLETON_ID = 'singleton';
const SESSION_DURATION_DAYS = 365;
const SESSION_DURATION_MS = SESSION_DURATION_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

export const SESSION_COOKIE_NAME = 'newlife_session';
export const SESSION_MAX_AGE_SECONDS = SESSION_DURATION_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60;

export function getSessionCookieOptions(isSecureCookie: boolean) {
	return {
		path: '/',
		httpOnly: true,
		sameSite: 'lax' as const,
		secure: isSecureCookie,
		maxAge: SESSION_MAX_AGE_SECONDS
	};
}

async function hashPasswordWithSalt(password: string, saltHex: string): Promise<string> {
	const derivedKey = (await scryptAsync(password, Buffer.from(saltHex, 'hex'), 64)) as Buffer;
	return derivedKey.toString('hex');
}

function hashSessionToken(rawToken: string): string {
	return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(rawToken).digest('hex');
}

export async function isPasswordConfigured(): Promise<boolean> {
	await ensureDatabaseReady();
	const rows = await db
		.select()
		.from(appAuth)
		.where(eq(appAuth.id, AUTH_SINGLETON_ID))
		.limit(1);
	return Boolean(rows[0]);
}

export async function setupInitialPassword(password: string): Promise<void> {
	await ensureDatabaseReady();
	const saltHex = crypto.randomBytes(16).toString('hex');
	const passwordHash = await hashPasswordWithSalt(password, saltHex);
	const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

	await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
		const existing = await tx
			.select()
			.from(appAuth)
			.where(eq(appAuth.id, AUTH_SINGLETON_ID))
			.limit(1);

		if (existing[0]) {
			throw new Error('رمز عبور قبلاً تنظیم شده است');
		}

		await tx.insert(appAuth).values({
			id: AUTH_SINGLETON_ID,
			passwordHash,
			passwordSalt: saltHex,
			updatedAtIso: nowIso
		});
	});
}

export async function verifyPassword(password: string): Promise<boolean> {
	await ensureDatabaseReady();
	const rows = await db
		.select()
		.from(appAuth)
		.where(eq(appAuth.id, AUTH_SINGLETON_ID))
		.limit(1);

	const record = rows[0];
	if (!record) return false;

	const candidateHash = await hashPasswordWithSalt(password, record.passwordSalt);
	const expectedBuf = Buffer.from(record.passwordHash, 'hex');
	const candidateBuf = Buffer.from(candidateHash, 'hex');

	if (expectedBuf.length !== candidateBuf.length) return false;
	return crypto.timingSafeEqual(expectedBuf, candidateBuf);
}

export async function changeMasterPassword(
	newPassword: string,
	keepRawToken?: string | null
): Promise<void> {
	await ensureDatabaseReady();
	const saltHex = crypto.randomBytes(16).toString('hex');
	const passwordHash = await hashPasswordWithSalt(newPassword, saltHex);
	const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

	await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
		await tx
			.update(appAuth)
			.set({
				passwordHash,
				passwordSalt: saltHex,
				updatedAtIso: nowIso
			})
			.where(eq(appAuth.id, AUTH_SINGLETON_ID));

		if (keepRawToken) {
			const currentSessionId = hashSessionToken(keepRawToken);
			await tx.delete(authSessions).where(ne(authSessions.id, currentSessionId));
		} else {
			await tx.delete(authSessions);
		}
	});
}

export async function createAuthSession(): Promise<{
	token: string;
	expiresAt: Date;
}> {
	await ensureDatabaseReady();
	const now = new Date();
	const nowIso = now.toISOString();
	const expiresAt = new Date(now.getTime() + SESSION_DURATION_MS);
	const expiresAtIso = expiresAt.toISOString();

	await db.delete(authSessions).where(lt(authSessions.expiresAtIso, nowIso));

	const rawToken = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
	const sessionId = hashSessionToken(rawToken);

	await db.insert(authSessions).values({
		id: sessionId,
		createdAtIso: nowIso,
		expiresAtIso,
		lastSeenAtIso: nowIso
	});

	return { token: rawToken, expiresAt };
}

export async function validateAndExtendSession(
	rawToken: string,
	forceRefresh = false
): Promise<{
	valid: boolean;
	shouldRefreshCookie: boolean;
	expiresAt: Date;
}> {
	await ensureDatabaseReady();
	const now = new Date();
	const nowIso = now.toISOString();
	const sessionId = hashSessionToken(rawToken);

	const rows = await db
		.select()
		.from(authSessions)
		.where(eq(authSessions.id, sessionId))
		.limit(1);

	const session = rows[0];
	if (!session) {
		return { valid: false, shouldRefreshCookie: false, expiresAt: now };
	}

	if (session.expiresAtIso <= nowIso) {
		await db.delete(authSessions).where(eq(authSessions.id, sessionId));
		return { valid: false, shouldRefreshCookie: false, expiresAt: now };
	}

	const newExpiresAt = new Date(now.getTime() + SESSION_DURATION_MS);
	const lastSeenMs = new Date(session.lastSeenAtIso).getTime();
	const hoursSinceLastSeen = (now.getTime() - lastSeenMs) / (1000 * 60 * 60);

	const shouldRefreshCookie = forceRefresh || hoursSinceLastSeen >= 1;
	if (shouldRefreshCookie) {
		await db
			.update(authSessions)
			.set({
				expiresAtIso: newExpiresAt.toISOString(),
				lastSeenAtIso: nowIso
			})
			.where(eq(authSessions.id, sessionId));
	}

	return {
		valid: true,
		shouldRefreshCookie,
		expiresAt: newExpiresAt
	};
}

export async function revokeAuthSession(rawToken: string): Promise<void> {
	await ensureDatabaseReady();
	const sessionId = hashSessionToken(rawToken);
	await db.delete(authSessions).where(eq(authSessions.id, sessionId));
}

================================================================
PATH: server/repositories/daily-tracker.repo.ts
================================================================

import { and, asc, eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { db, ensureDatabaseReady } from '../database/index';
import {
	cadencePlans,
	dailyEntries,
	habitLogs,
	habits,
	reflectionTags
} from '../database/schema';
import { getAllHabits } from './habits.repo';
import { bumpServerStructuralVersion } from './sync.repo';
import {
	isDailyEntryEmpty,
	resolveHabitRuleSnapshot,
	serializeHabitSpecSnapshot
} from '~/core/habit-engine';
import { DEFAULT_REFLECTION_TAGS } from '~/domain/constants';
import type {
	HabitEntity,
	SaveCadencePlanBatch,
	SaveDailyEntryInput
} from '~/domain/schemas';

export async function getReflectionTags(): Promise<string[]> {
	await ensureDatabaseReady();
	const rows = await db
		.select()
		.from(reflectionTags)
		.orderBy(asc(reflectionTags.sortOrder));

	if (rows.length === 0) {
		return [...DEFAULT_REFLECTION_TAGS];
	}
	return rows.map((r) => r.label);
}

export type DbExecutor = Pick<typeof db, 'select' | 'insert' | 'update' | 'delete'>;

export async function saveDailyEntryAndLogs(
	input: SaveDailyEntryInput,
	preloadedHabitMap?: ReadonlyMap<string, HabitEntity>,
	txExecutor?: DbExecutor
): Promise<void> {
	await ensureDatabaseReady();
	const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
	const entryUpdatedAtIso = input.updatedAtIso || nowIso;
	const normalizedJournal = input.journalText?.trim() ? input.journalText.trim() : null;
	const habitMap =
		preloadedHabitMap ?? new Map((await getAllHabits()).map((h) => [h.id, h]));

	const applyOperations = async (tx: DbExecutor) => {
		const existingRows = await tx
			.select()
			.from(dailyEntries)
			.where(eq(dailyEntries.dateIso, input.dateIso))
			.limit(1);
		const existing = existingRows[0];

		if (existing) {
			if (!input.updatedAtIso || entryUpdatedAtIso >= existing.updatedAtIso) {
				await tx
					.update(dailyEntries)
					.set({
						dayStatus: input.dayStatus,
						reflectionNote: input.reflectionNote,
						journalText: normalizedJournal,
						tagsJson: JSON.stringify(input.tags),
						updatedAtIso: entryUpdatedAtIso
					})
					.where(eq(dailyEntries.dateIso, input.dateIso));
			}
		} else {
			await tx.insert(dailyEntries).values({
				id: `entry:${input.dateIso}`,
				dateIso: input.dateIso,
				dayStatus: input.dayStatus,
				reflectionNote: input.reflectionNote,
				journalText: normalizedJournal,
				tagsJson: JSON.stringify(input.tags),
				updatedAtIso: entryUpdatedAtIso
			});
		}

		if (isDailyEntryEmpty(input)) {
			await tx.delete(habitLogs).where(eq(habitLogs.dateIso, input.dateIso));
			await tx.delete(dailyEntries).where(eq(dailyEntries.dateIso, input.dateIso));
			bumpServerStructuralVersion(entryUpdatedAtIso);
			return;
		}

		const previousLogs = await tx
			.select()
			.from(habitLogs)
			.where(eq(habitLogs.dateIso, input.dateIso));
		const previousLogByHabitId = new Map(previousLogs.map((l) => [l.habitId, l]));

		for (const log of input.logs) {
			const habit = habitMap.get(log.habitId);
			const prev = previousLogByHabitId.get(log.habitId);

			const isUnloggedTiered =
				habit?.trackingMode === 'tiered' &&
				(log.achievedTier === null || log.achievedTier === undefined);

			if (isUnloggedTiered) {
				if (prev) {
					await tx.delete(habitLogs).where(eq(habitLogs.id, prev.id));
					bumpServerStructuralVersion(entryUpdatedAtIso);
				}
				continue;
			}

			if (
				prev?.updatedAtIso &&
				log.updatedAtIso &&
				log.updatedAtIso < prev.updatedAtIso
			) {
				continue;
			}

			const logUpdatedAt = log.updatedAtIso || nowIso;
			const tierUnchanged = prev && prev.achievedTier === log.achievedTier;

			const habitTitleSnapshot =
				(tierUnchanged && prev?.habitTitleSnapshot) ||
				log.habitTitleSnapshot ||
				habit?.title ||
				prev?.habitTitleSnapshot ||
				'طرح';

			const achievedRuleSnapshot =
				(tierUnchanged && prev?.achievedRuleSnapshot) ||
				(habit ? resolveHabitRuleSnapshot(habit, log.achievedTier) : null) ||
				log.achievedRuleSnapshot ||
				prev?.achievedRuleSnapshot ||
				null;

			const habitSnapshotJson =
				(tierUnchanged && prev?.habitSnapshotJson) ||
				(habit
					? serializeHabitSpecSnapshot(habit)
					: (prev?.habitSnapshotJson ?? null));

			if (prev) {
				await tx
					.update(habitLogs)
					.set({
						habitTitleSnapshot,
						achievedTier: log.achievedTier,
						achievedRuleSnapshot,
						habitSnapshotJson,
						completedIntervals: log.completedIntervals,
						actualMinutesPerInterval: log.actualMinutesPerInterval,
						totalActualMinutes: log.totalActualMinutes,
						plannedIntervalsSnapshot:
							log.plannedIntervalsSnapshot ?? prev.plannedIntervalsSnapshot ?? null,
						plannedMinutesSnapshot:
							log.plannedMinutesSnapshot ?? prev.plannedMinutesSnapshot ?? null,
						updatedAtIso: logUpdatedAt
					})
					.where(eq(habitLogs.id, prev.id));
			} else {
				await tx.insert(habitLogs).values({
					id: `log:${input.dateIso}:${log.habitId}`,
					dateIso: input.dateIso,
					habitId: log.habitId,
					habitTitleSnapshot,
					achievedTier: log.achievedTier,
					achievedRuleSnapshot,
					habitSnapshotJson,
					completedIntervals: log.completedIntervals,
					actualMinutesPerInterval: log.actualMinutesPerInterval,
					totalActualMinutes: log.totalActualMinutes,
					plannedIntervalsSnapshot: log.plannedIntervalsSnapshot ?? null,
					plannedMinutesSnapshot: log.plannedMinutesSnapshot ?? null,
					updatedAtIso: logUpdatedAt
				});
			}
		}
	};

	if (txExecutor) {
		await applyOperations(txExecutor);
	} else {
		await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
			await applyOperations(tx);
		});
	}
}

export async function saveCadencePlansForDate(
	input: SaveCadencePlanBatch,
	preloadedHabitMap?: Map<string, HabitEntity>,
	txExecutor?: DbExecutor
): Promise<void> {
	await ensureDatabaseReady();
	const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
	const habitMap =
		preloadedHabitMap ?? new Map((await getAllHabits()).map((h) => [h.id, h]));

	const applyOperations = async (tx: DbExecutor) => {
		for (const item of input.items) {
			const existingRows = await tx
				.select()
				.from(cadencePlans)
				.where(
					and(
						eq(cadencePlans.dateIso, input.targetDateIso),
						eq(cadencePlans.habitId, item.habitId)
					)
				)
				.limit(1);
			const existing = existingRows[0];

			if (existing) {
				await tx
					.update(cadencePlans)
					.set({
						plannedIntervals: item.plannedIntervals,
						plannedMinutesPerInterval: item.plannedMinutesPerInterval,
						cadenceWindowMinutes: item.cadenceWindowMinutes,
						updatedAtIso: nowIso
					})
					.where(eq(cadencePlans.id, existing.id));
			} else {
				await tx.insert(cadencePlans).values({
					id: `plan:${input.targetDateIso}:${item.habitId}`,
					dateIso: input.targetDateIso,
					habitId: item.habitId,
					plannedIntervals: item.plannedIntervals,
					plannedMinutesPerInterval: item.plannedMinutesPerInterval,
					cadenceWindowMinutes: item.cadenceWindowMinutes,
					updatedAtIso: nowIso
				});
			}

			const habit = habitMap.get(item.habitId);
			if (
				habit &&
				(habit.defaultIntervals !== item.plannedIntervals ||
					habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval !== item.plannedMinutesPerInterval ||
					habit.cadenceWindowMinutes !== item.cadenceWindowMinutes)
			) {
				await tx
					.update(habits)
					.set({
						defaultIntervals: item.plannedIntervals,
						defaultMinutesPerInterval: item.plannedMinutesPerInterval,
						cadenceWindowMinutes: item.cadenceWindowMinutes,
						updatedAtIso: nowIso
					})
					.where(eq(habits.id, habit.id));

				habitMap.set(habit.id, {
					...habit,
					defaultIntervals: item.plannedIntervals,
					defaultMinutesPerInterval: item.plannedMinutesPerInterval,
					cadenceWindowMinutes: item.cadenceWindowMinutes,
					updatedAtIso: nowIso
				});
			}
		}
	};

	if (txExecutor) {
		await applyOperations(txExecutor);
	} else {
		await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
			await applyOperations(tx);
		});
	}
}


----------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================
PATH: server/repositories/backup.repo.ts
================================================================

import { like } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { PgColumn, PgTable } from 'drizzle-orm/pg-core';
import { db, ensureDatabaseReady } from '../database/index';
import {
	books,
	cadencePlans,
	calendarEvents,
	dailyEntries,
	habitLifecycleEvents,
	habitLogs,
	habits,
	mediaLogs,
	notes,
	reflectionTags
} from '../database/schema';
import { bumpServerStructuralVersion } from './sync.repo';
import { computeSmartBackupMerge } from '~/core/backup-merger';
import { DEMO_ENTRY_TAG, DEMO_ID_PREFIX } from '~/domain/demo-seed';
import type { DatabaseBackupPayload } from '~/domain/schemas';

export async function clearAllDatabaseRecords(): Promise<void> {
	await ensureDatabaseReady();
	await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
		await tx.delete(habitLogs);
		await tx.delete(dailyEntries);
		await tx.delete(cadencePlans);
		await tx.delete(habitLifecycleEvents);
		await tx.delete(reflectionTags);
		await tx.delete(habits);
		await tx.delete(books);
		await tx.delete(mediaLogs);
		await tx.delete(calendarEvents);
		await tx.delete(notes);
	});

	bumpServerStructuralVersion();
}

export async function removeDemoSeedFromServer(): Promise<{ removedRecordsCount: number }> {
	await ensureDatabaseReady();
	let removed = 0;
	const prefixPattern = `${DEMO_ID_PREFIX}%`;
	const tagPattern = `%${DEMO_ENTRY_TAG}%`;

	await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
		removed += (
			await tx
				.delete(habitLogs)
				.where(like(habitLogs.id, prefixPattern))
				.returning({ id: habitLogs.id })
		).length;
		removed += (
			await tx
				.delete(habitLogs)
				.where(like(habitLogs.habitId, prefixPattern))
				.returning({ id: habitLogs.id })
		).length;
		removed += (
			await tx
				.delete(dailyEntries)
				.where(like(dailyEntries.id, prefixPattern))
				.returning({ id: dailyEntries.id })
		).length;
		removed += (
			await tx
				.delete(dailyEntries)
				.where(like(dailyEntries.tagsJson, tagPattern))
				.returning({ id: dailyEntries.id })
		).length;
		removed += (
			await tx
				.delete(cadencePlans)
				.where(like(cadencePlans.id, prefixPattern))
				.returning({ id: cadencePlans.id })
		).length;
		removed += (
			await tx
				.delete(habitLifecycleEvents)
				.where(like(habitLifecycleEvents.id, prefixPattern))
				.returning({ id: habitLifecycleEvents.id })
		).length;
		removed += (
			await tx
				.delete(habits)
				.where(like(habits.id, prefixPattern))
				.returning({ id: habits.id })
		).length;
		removed += (
			await tx
				.delete(books)
				.where(like(books.id, prefixPattern))
				.returning({ id: books.id })
		).length;
		removed += (
			await tx
				.delete(mediaLogs)
				.where(like(mediaLogs.id, prefixPattern))
				.returning({ id: mediaLogs.id })
		).length;
		removed += (
			await tx
				.delete(notes)
				.where(like(notes.id, prefixPattern))
				.returning({ id: notes.id })
		).length;
		removed += (
			await tx
				.delete(calendarEvents)
				.where(like(calendarEvents.id, prefixPattern))
				.returning({ id: calendarEvents.id })
		).length;
	});

	bumpServerStructuralVersion();
	return { removedRecordsCount: removed };
}

export async function importDatabaseFromJson(
	payload: DatabaseBackupPayload,
	mode: 'replace' | 'merge' = 'merge'
): Promise<{ importedRecordsCount: number; skippedDuplicatesCount: number }> {
	await ensureDatabaseReady();
	const { data } = payload;
	let totalImported = 0;
	let skippedDuplicatesCount = 0;

	await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
		if (mode === 'replace') {
			await tx.delete(habitLogs);
			await tx.delete(dailyEntries);
			await tx.delete(cadencePlans);
			await tx.delete(habitLifecycleEvents);
			await tx.delete(reflectionTags);
			await tx.delete(habits);
			await tx.delete(books);
			await tx.delete(mediaLogs);
			await tx.delete(calendarEvents);
			await tx.delete(notes);
		}

		const existingData = {
			habits: await tx.select().from(habits),
			dailyEntries: await tx.select().from(dailyEntries),
			habitLogs: await tx.select().from(habitLogs),
			cadencePlans: await tx.select().from(cadencePlans),
			books: await tx.select().from(books),
			media: await tx.select().from(mediaLogs),
			notes: await tx.select().from(notes),
			calendarEvents: await tx.select().from(calendarEvents),
			lifecycleEvents: await tx.select().from(habitLifecycleEvents),
			reflectionTags: await tx.select().from(reflectionTags)
		};

		const merged = computeSmartBackupMerge(existingData, data);
		skippedDuplicatesCount = merged.skippedDuplicatesCount;

		const upsertRows = async <T extends Record<string, unknown>>(
			table: PgTable,
			targetCol: PgColumn,
			rows: ReadonlyArray<T>
		) => {
			for (const row of rows) {
				await tx
					.insert(table)
					.values(row as Record<string, unknown>)
					.onConflictDoUpdate({
						target: targetCol,
						set: row as Record<string, unknown>
					});
				totalImported++;
			}
		};

		await upsertRows(habits, habits.id, merged.habitsToUpsert);

		for (const e of merged.dailyEntriesToUpsert) {
			const { id: _id, ...entryFields } = e;
			await tx
				.insert(dailyEntries)
				.values(e)
				.onConflictDoUpdate({
					target: dailyEntries.dateIso,
					set: entryFields
				});
			totalImported++;
		}

		for (const l of merged.habitLogsToUpsert) {
			const { id: _id, ...logFields } = l;
			await tx
				.insert(habitLogs)
				.values(l)
				.onConflictDoUpdate({
					target: [habitLogs.dateIso, habitLogs.habitId],
					set: logFields
				});
			totalImported++;
		}

		for (const c of merged.cadencePlansToUpsert) {
			const { id: _id, ...planFields } = c;
			await tx
				.insert(cadencePlans)
				.values(c)
				.onConflictDoUpdate({
					target: [cadencePlans.dateIso, cadencePlans.habitId],
					set: planFields
				});
			totalImported++;
		}

		await upsertRows(books, books.id, merged.booksToUpsert);
		await upsertRows(mediaLogs, mediaLogs.id, merged.mediaToUpsert);
		await upsertRows(notes, notes.id, merged.notesToUpsert);
		await upsertRows(calendarEvents, calendarEvents.id, merged.calendarEventsToUpsert);
		await upsertRows(
			habitLifecycleEvents,
			habitLifecycleEvents.id,
			merged.lifecycleEventsToUpsert
		);

		for (const tag of merged.reflectionTagsToUpsert) {
			const { id: _id, ...tagFields } = tag;
			await tx
				.insert(reflectionTags)
				.values(tag)
				.onConflictDoUpdate({
					target: reflectionTags.label,
					set: tagFields
				});
			totalImported++;
		}
	});

	bumpServerStructuralVersion();
	return { importedRecordsCount: totalImported, skippedDuplicatesCount };
}

================================================================
PATH: server/repositories/sync.repo.ts
================================================================

import { asc, desc, eq, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { db, ensureDatabaseReady } from '../database/index';
import {
	books,
	cadencePlans,
	calendarEvents,
	dailyEntries,
	habitLifecycleEvents,
	habitLogs,
	habits,
	mediaLogs,
	notes
} from '../database/schema';
import { getAllHabits } from './habits.repo';
import {
	getReflectionTags,
	saveCadencePlansForDate,
	saveDailyEntryAndLogs
} from './daily-tracker.repo';
import { parseTagsJson } from '~/domain/tags';
import type {
	LocalWorkspaceSnapshot,
	ServerSyncResponse,
	SyncRequestPayload
} from '~/domain/schemas';

let lastStructuralMutationIso = '1970-01-01T00:00:00.000Z';

export function bumpServerStructuralVersion(iso = new Date().toISOString()): void {
	if (iso > lastStructuralMutationIso) {
		lastStructuralMutationIso = iso;
	}
}

export async function getServerMaxVersionIso(): Promise<string> {
	await ensureDatabaseReady();
	const rows = await db.execute<{ max_iso: string | null }>(sql`
		SELECT MAX(ts) AS max_iso FROM (
			SELECT MAX(updated_at_iso) AS ts FROM daily_entries
			UNION ALL
			SELECT MAX(updated_at_iso) AS ts FROM habit_logs
			UNION ALL
			SELECT MAX(updated_at_iso) AS ts FROM habits
			UNION ALL
			SELECT MAX(updated_at_iso) AS ts FROM cadence_plans
			UNION ALL
			SELECT MAX(updated_at_iso) AS ts FROM notes
			UNION ALL
			SELECT MAX(updated_at_iso) AS ts FROM books
			UNION ALL
			SELECT MAX(updated_at_iso) AS ts FROM media_logs
			UNION ALL
			SELECT MAX(updated_at_iso) AS ts FROM calendar_events
			UNION ALL
			SELECT MAX(created_at_iso) AS ts FROM habit_lifecycle_events
		) AS combined_timestamps
	`);

	const dbMax = rows[0]?.max_iso || '1970-01-01T00:00:00.000Z';
	return dbMax > lastStructuralMutationIso ? dbMax : lastStructuralMutationIso;
}

export async function processClientSyncRequest(
	input: SyncRequestPayload,
	appVersion = '0.0.1'
): Promise<ServerSyncResponse> {
	await ensureDatabaseReady();
	const versionBeforeMutations = await getServerMaxVersionIso();
	const hadRemoteUpdatesBeforePush = Boolean(
		input.knownServerVersionIso && versionBeforeMutations > input.knownServerVersionIso
	);

	const appliedMutationIds: string[] = [];

	if (input.mutations.length > 0) {
		const sortedMutations = [...input.mutations].sort((a, b) =>
			a.queuedAtIso > b.queuedAtIso ? 1 : a.queuedAtIso < b.queuedAtIso ? -1 : 0
		);

		const allHabitsList = await getAllHabits();
		const preloadedHabitMap = new Map(allHabitsList.map((h) => [h.id, h]));

		for (const mutation of sortedMutations) {
			try {
				if (mutation.kind === 'SAVE_DAILY_ENTRY') {
					await saveDailyEntryAndLogs(mutation.payload, preloadedHabitMap);
				} else if (mutation.kind === 'SAVE_CADENCE_PLAN') {
					await saveCadencePlansForDate(mutation.payload, preloadedHabitMap);
				} else if (mutation.kind === 'UPSERT_BOOK') {
					await db
						.insert(books)
						.values(mutation.payload)
						.onConflictDoUpdate({
							target: books.id,
							set: mutation.payload
						});
				} else if (mutation.kind === 'DELETE_BOOK') {
					await db.delete(books).where(eq(books.id, mutation.payload.bookId));
					bumpServerStructuralVersion(mutation.queuedAtIso);
				} else if (mutation.kind === 'UPSERT_MEDIA') {
					await db
						.insert(mediaLogs)
						.values(mutation.payload)
						.onConflictDoUpdate({
							target: mediaLogs.id,
							set: mutation.payload
						});
				} else if (mutation.kind === 'DELETE_MEDIA') {
					await db
						.delete(mediaLogs)
						.where(eq(mediaLogs.id, mutation.payload.mediaId));
					bumpServerStructuralVersion(mutation.queuedAtIso);
				} else if (mutation.kind === 'UPSERT_NOTE') {
					await db
						.insert(notes)
						.values(mutation.payload)
						.onConflictDoUpdate({
							target: notes.id,
							set: mutation.payload
						});
				} else if (mutation.kind === 'DELETE_NOTE') {
					await db.delete(notes).where(eq(notes.id, mutation.payload.noteId));
					bumpServerStructuralVersion(mutation.queuedAtIso);
				} else if (mutation.kind === 'UPSERT_CALENDAR_EVENT') {
					await db
						.insert(calendarEvents)
						.values(mutation.payload)
						.onConflictDoUpdate({
							target: calendarEvents.id,
							set: mutation.payload
						});
				} else if (mutation.kind === 'DELETE_CALENDAR_EVENT') {
					await db
						.delete(calendarEvents)
						.where(eq(calendarEvents.id, mutation.payload.eventId));
					bumpServerStructuralVersion(mutation.queuedAtIso);
				} else if (mutation.kind === 'UPSERT_HABIT') {
					await db
						.insert(habits)
						.values(mutation.payload)
						.onConflictDoUpdate({
							target: habits.id,
							set: mutation.payload
						});
					preloadedHabitMap.set(mutation.payload.id, mutation.payload);
				} else if (mutation.kind === 'ADD_LIFECYCLE_EVENT') {
					await db
						.insert(habitLifecycleEvents)
						.values(mutation.payload)
						.onConflictDoUpdate({
							target: habitLifecycleEvents.id,
							set: mutation.payload
						});
				}
				appliedMutationIds.push(mutation.id);
			} catch (err) {
				console.error(
					`[Sync] Failed to apply mutation ${mutation.id} (${mutation.kind}):`,
					err
				);
				appliedMutationIds.push(mutation.id);
			}
		}
	}

	const serverVersionIso = await getServerMaxVersionIso();
	const serverTimeIso = new Date().toISOString();
	const shouldSendFullWorkspace =
		!input.knownServerVersionIso || hadRemoteUpdatesBeforePush;

	if (!shouldSendFullWorkspace) {
		return {
			ok: true,
			appliedMutationIds,
			serverVersionIso,
			appBuildVersion: appVersion,
			hasRemoteUpdates: false,
			serverTimeIso
		};
	}

	const [
		allEntriesRaw,
		allLogsRaw,
		allHabits,
		availableTags,
		allCadencePlans,
		allBooks,
		allMedia,
		allNotes,
		allCalendarEvents,
		allLifecycleEvents
	] = await Promise.all([
		db.select().from(dailyEntries).orderBy(desc(dailyEntries.dateIso)),
		db.select().from(habitLogs).orderBy(desc(habitLogs.dateIso)),
		getAllHabits(),
		getReflectionTags(),
		db.select().from(cadencePlans).orderBy(asc(cadencePlans.dateIso)),
		db.select().from(books).orderBy(desc(books.createdAtIso)),
		db.select().from(mediaLogs).orderBy(desc(mediaLogs.createdAtIso)),
		db
			.select()
			.from(notes)
			.orderBy(desc(notes.isPinned), desc(notes.updatedAtIso)),
		db.select().from(calendarEvents).orderBy(desc(calendarEvents.createdAtIso)),
		db
			.select()
			.from(habitLifecycleEvents)
			.orderBy(desc(habitLifecycleEvents.createdAtIso))
	]);

	const mappedEntries = allEntriesRaw.map((e) => ({
		id: e.id,
		dateIso: e.dateIso,
		dayStatus: e.dayStatus,
		reflectionNote: e.reflectionNote,
		journalText: e.journalText ?? null,
		tags: parseTagsJson(e.tagsJson),
		updatedAtIso: e.updatedAtIso
	}));

	const mappedLogs = allLogsRaw.map((l) => ({
		id: l.id,
		dateIso: l.dateIso,
		habitId: l.habitId,
		habitTitleSnapshot: l.habitTitleSnapshot,
		achievedTier: l.achievedTier,
		achievedRuleSnapshot: l.achievedRuleSnapshot,
		habitSnapshotJson: l.habitSnapshotJson ?? null,
		completedIntervals: l.completedIntervals,
		actualMinutesPerInterval: l.actualMinutesPerInterval,
		totalActualMinutes: l.totalActualMinutes,
		plannedIntervalsSnapshot: l.plannedIntervalsSnapshot,
		plannedMinutesSnapshot: l.plannedMinutesSnapshot,
		updatedAtIso: l.updatedAtIso ?? null
	}));

	const fullWorkspace: LocalWorkspaceSnapshot = {
		versionIso: serverVersionIso,
		syncedAtIso: serverTimeIso,
		habits: allHabits,
		availableTags,
		entries: mappedEntries,
		logs: mappedLogs,
		cadencePlans: allCadencePlans,
		books: allBooks,
		media: allMedia,
		notes: allNotes,
		calendarEvents: allCalendarEvents,
		lifecycleEvents: allLifecycleEvents
	};

	return {
		ok: true,
		appliedMutationIds,
		serverVersionIso,
		appBuildVersion: appVersion,
		hasRemoteUpdates: hadRemoteUpdatesBeforePush,
		serverTimeIso,
		fullWorkspace
	};
}

================================================================
PATH: server/middleware/auth.ts
================================================================

import { defineEventHandler, getCookie, setCookie, deleteCookie, createError } from 'h3';
import {
	isPasswordConfigured,
	validateAndExtendSession,
	getSessionCookieOptions,
	SESSION_COOKIE_NAME
} from '../repositories/auth.repo';

export default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {
	const pathname = event.path || '';

	if (!pathname.startsWith('/api/')) {
		return;
	}

	const isPublicAuthApi =
		pathname === '/api/auth/login' ||
		pathname === '/api/auth/setup' ||
		pathname === '/api/auth/status' ||
		pathname === '/api/auth/logout';

	if (isPublicAuthApi) {
		return;
	}

	const passwordConfigured = await isPasswordConfigured();
	const rawToken = getCookie(event, SESSION_COOKIE_NAME) || null;
	let isAuthenticated = false;

	const isSecure = process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production';

	if (passwordConfigured && rawToken) {
		const sessionStatus = await validateAndExtendSession(rawToken);
		isAuthenticated = sessionStatus.valid;

		if (sessionStatus.valid && sessionStatus.shouldRefreshCookie) {
			setCookie(event, SESSION_COOKIE_NAME, rawToken, getSessionCookieOptions(isSecure));
		} else if (!sessionStatus.valid) {
			deleteCookie(event, SESSION_COOKIE_NAME, { path: '/' });
		}
	}

	event.context.isAuthenticated = isAuthenticated;
	event.context.isPasswordConfigured = passwordConfigured;
	event.context.sessionToken = rawToken;

	if (passwordConfigured && !isAuthenticated) {
		throw createError({
			statusCode: 401,
			statusMessage: 'ابتدا وارد حساب کاربری شوید'
		});
	}
});

import { drizzle } from 'drizzle-orm/postgres-js';
import postgres from 'postgres';
import * as schema from './schema';

const connectionString =
	process.env.DATABASE_URL ||
	'postgresql://postgres:postgres@localhost:5432/personal_os';

const globalForPg = globalThis as unknown as {
	__personalOsPgClient?: ReturnType<typeof postgres>;
};

export const pgClient =
	globalForPg.__personalOsPgClient ??
	postgres(connectionString, {
		max: 10,
		idle_timeout: 20,
		max_lifetime: 60 * 30,
		connect_timeout: 10,
		prepare: false,
		onnotice: () => {}
	});

if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') {
	globalForPg.__personalOsPgClient = pgClient;
}

export const db = drizzle(pgClient, { schema });

let schemaInitPromise: Promise<void> | null = null;

async function bootstrapPostgreSqlSchema(): Promise<void> {
	await pgClient.unsafe(`
		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS habits (
			id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			parent_id TEXT,
			title TEXT NOT NULL,
			category TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'عمومی',
			tracking_mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'tiered',
			time_slot TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'day',
			is_situational BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
			status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
			schedule_label TEXT,
			notes TEXT,
			bronze_desc TEXT,
			silver_desc TEXT,
			gold_desc TEXT,
			emergency_min_desc TEXT,
			default_intervals INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 2,
			default_minutes_per_interval DOUBLE PRECISION NOT NULL DEFAULT 10,
			cadence_window_minutes INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 60,
			sort_order INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
			created_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL,
			updated_at_iso TEXT
		);

		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cadence_plans (
			id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			date_iso TEXT NOT NULL,
			habit_id TEXT NOT NULL,
			planned_intervals INTEGER NOT NULL,
			planned_minutes_per_interval DOUBLE PRECISION NOT NULL,
			cadence_window_minutes INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 60,
			updated_at_iso TEXT
		);

		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS daily_entries (
			id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			date_iso TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
			day_status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'optimal',
			reflection_note TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
			journal_text TEXT,
			tags_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',
			updated_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL
		);

		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS habit_logs (
			id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			date_iso TEXT NOT NULL,
			habit_id TEXT NOT NULL,
			habit_title_snapshot TEXT,
			achieved_tier INTEGER,
			achieved_rule_snapshot TEXT,
			habit_snapshot_json TEXT,
			completed_intervals INTEGER,
			actual_minutes_per_interval DOUBLE PRECISION,
			total_actual_minutes DOUBLE PRECISION,
			planned_intervals_snapshot INTEGER,
			planned_minutes_snapshot DOUBLE PRECISION,
			updated_at_iso TEXT
		);

		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS habit_lifecycle_events (
			id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			habit_id TEXT,
			habit_title_snapshot TEXT NOT NULL,
			event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
			effective_date_iso TEXT NOT NULL,
			summary TEXT NOT NULL,
			reason TEXT,
			previous_state_json TEXT,
			new_state_json TEXT,
			created_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL
		);

		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS reflection_tags (
			id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			label TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
			sort_order INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
		);

		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS books (
			id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			title TEXT NOT NULL,
			author TEXT,
			status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'completed',
			started_at_iso TEXT,
			finished_at_iso TEXT,
			rating INTEGER DEFAULT 5,
			notes TEXT,
			created_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL,
			updated_at_iso TEXT
		);

		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS media_logs (
			id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			title TEXT NOT NULL,
			category TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'movie',
			status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'completed',
			started_at_iso TEXT,
			finished_at_iso TEXT,
			rating INTEGER DEFAULT 5,
			notes TEXT,
			created_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL,
			updated_at_iso TEXT
		);

		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS calendar_events (
			id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			title TEXT NOT NULL,
			event_type TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'occasion',
			is_annual BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
			date_iso TEXT,
			jalali_month INTEGER NOT NULL,
			jalali_day INTEGER NOT NULL,
			is_completed BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
			notes TEXT,
			created_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL,
			updated_at_iso TEXT
		);

		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS notes (
			id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			title TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
			content TEXT NOT NULL,
			category TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'عمومی',
			is_pinned BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
			created_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL,
			updated_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL
		);

		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS app_auth (
			id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
			password_salt TEXT NOT NULL,
			updated_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL
		);

		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS auth_sessions (
			id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			created_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL,
			expires_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL,
			last_seen_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL
		);

		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_habits_parent_id ON habits(parent_id);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_habits_updated_at ON habits(updated_at_iso);
		CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_cadence_plans_date_habit ON cadence_plans(date_iso, habit_id);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_cadence_plans_updated_at ON cadence_plans(updated_at_iso);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_daily_entries_updated_at ON daily_entries(updated_at_iso);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_habit_logs_date_iso ON habit_logs(date_iso);
		CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_habit_logs_date_habit ON habit_logs(date_iso, habit_id);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_habit_logs_updated_at ON habit_logs(updated_at_iso);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_lifecycle_events_habit_id ON habit_lifecycle_events(habit_id);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_books_updated_at ON books(updated_at_iso);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_books_dates ON books(started_at_iso, finished_at_iso);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_media_logs_updated_at ON media_logs(updated_at_iso);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_media_logs_dates ON media_logs(started_at_iso, finished_at_iso);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_calendar_events_updated_at ON calendar_events(updated_at_iso);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_calendar_events_date ON calendar_events(date_iso);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_calendar_events_jalali ON calendar_events(jalali_month, jalali_day);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_notes_updated_at ON notes(updated_at_iso);
		CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_auth_sessions_expires_at ON auth_sessions(expires_at_iso);
	`);
}

export async function ensureDatabaseReady(): Promise<void> {
	if (!schemaInitPromise) {
		schemaInitPromise = bootstrapPostgreSqlSchema().catch((err) => {
			schemaInitPromise = null;
			throw err;
		});
	}
	return schemaInitPromise;
}

import {
	boolean,
	doublePrecision,
	index,
	integer,
	pgTable,
	text,
	uniqueIndex
} from 'drizzle-orm/pg-core';

export const habits = pgTable(
	'habits',
	{
		id: text('id').primaryKey(),
		parentId: text('parent_id'),
		title: text('title').notNull(),
		category: text('category').notNull().default('عمومی'),
		trackingMode: text('tracking_mode', { enum: ['tiered', 'interval_cadence'] })
			.notNull()
			.default('tiered'),
		timeSlot: text('time_slot', { enum: ['morning', 'day', 'night'] })
			.notNull()
			.default('day'),
		isSituational: boolean('is_situational').notNull().default(false),
		status: text('status', { enum: ['active', 'paused', 'archived'] })
			.notNull()
			.default('active'),
		scheduleLabel: text('schedule_label'),
		notes: text('notes'),
		bronzeDesc: text('bronze_desc'),
		silverDesc: text('silver_desc'),
		goldDesc: text('gold_desc'),
		emergencyMinDesc: text('emergency_min_desc'),
		defaultIntervals: integer('default_intervals').notNull().default(2),
		defaultMinutesPerInterval: doublePrecision('default_minutes_per_interval')
			.notNull()
			.default(10),
		cadenceWindowMinutes: integer('cadence_window_minutes').notNull().default(60),
		sortOrder: integer('sort_order').notNull().default(0),
		createdAtIso: text('created_at_iso').notNull(),
		updatedAtIso: text('updated_at_iso')
	},
	(t) => [
		index('idx_habits_parent_id').on(t.parentId),
		index('idx_habits_updated_at').on(t.updatedAtIso)
	]
);

import { defineEventHandler, readBody, createError } from 'h3';
import { DatabaseBackupPayloadSchema } from '~/domain/schemas';
import { importDatabaseFromJson } from '../../repositories/backup.repo';

export default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {
	const body = await readBody(event);
	const parsed = DatabaseBackupPayloadSchema.safeParse(body);
	if (!parsed.success) {
		throw createError({
			statusCode: 400,
			statusMessage: `فایل بکاپ نامعتبر است: ${parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? ''}`
		});
	}

	const result = await importDatabaseFromJson(parsed.data, 'merge');
	return {
		success: true,
		importedCount: result.importedRecordsCount,
		skippedCount: result.skippedDuplicatesCount
	};
});

PATH: server/api/backup/demo-seed.post.ts

import { defineEventHandler, readBody, createError } from 'h3';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { importDatabaseFromJson, removeDemoSeedFromServer } from '../../repositories/backup.repo';
import { buildDemoSeedDataset } from '~/domain/demo-seed';

const DemoSeedActionSchema = z.object({
	action: z.enum(['inject', 'remove'])
});

export default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {
	const body = await readBody(event);
	const parsed = DemoSeedActionSchema.safeParse(body);
	if (!parsed.success) {
		throw createError({ statusCode: 400, statusMessage: 'عملیات نامعتبر است' });
	}

	if (parsed.data.action === 'inject') {
		const dataset = buildDemoSeedDataset();
		const res = await importDatabaseFromJson(dataset, 'merge');
		return { ok: true, count: res.importedRecordsCount };
	}

	const res = await removeDemoSeedFromServer();
	return { ok: true, count: res.removedRecordsCount };
});

import { defineEventHandler, readBody, createError } from 'h3';
import { SyncRequestPayloadSchema } from '~/domain/schemas';
import { processClientSyncRequest } from '../repositories/sync.repo';

export default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {
	try {
		const rawBody = await readBody(event);
		const parsed = SyncRequestPayloadSchema.safeParse(rawBody);

		if (!parsed.success) {
			throw createError({
				statusCode: 400,
				statusMessage: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? 'فرمت بسته همگام‌سازی نامعتبر است'
			});
		}

		const config = useRuntimeConfig();
		const appVersion = (config.public?.appVersion as string) || '0.0.1';

		return await processClientSyncRequest(parsed.data, appVersion);
	} catch (err: unknown) {
		const error = err as { statusCode?: number; statusMessage?: string; message?: string };
		throw createError({
			statusCode: error.statusCode || 500,
			statusMessage: error.statusMessage || error.message || 'خطا در پردازش همگام‌سازی با سرور'
		});
	}
});

import { defineEventHandler, getCookie, deleteCookie } from 'h3';
import { revokeAuthSession, SESSION_COOKIE_NAME } from '../../repositories/auth.repo';

export default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {
	const token = getCookie(event, SESSION_COOKIE_NAME);
	if (token) {
		await revokeAuthSession(token);
	}
	deleteCookie(event, SESSION_COOKIE_NAME, { path: '/' });
	return { success: true };
});


----------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================
PATH: server/api/auth/login.post.ts
================================================================

import { defineEventHandler, readBody, setCookie, createError } from 'h3';
import { LoginInputSchema } from '~/domain/schemas';
import {
	verifyPassword,
	createAuthSession,
	getSessionCookieOptions,
	SESSION_COOKIE_NAME
} from '../../repositories/auth.repo';

const failedLoginAttemptsByIp = new Map<string, { failedCount: number; lastAttemptMs: number }>();
const RESET_WINDOW_MS = 30 * 60 * 1000;

function resolveProgressiveDelayMs(attemptNumber: number): number {
	if (attemptNumber <= 1) return 0;
	if (attemptNumber === 2) return 2000;
	if (attemptNumber === 3) return 3000;
	return 5000;
}

function sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
	if (ms <= 0) return Promise.resolve();
	return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

function pruneExpiredLoginAttempts(now: number): void {
	if (failedLoginAttemptsByIp.size < 100) return;
	for (const [key, entry] of failedLoginAttemptsByIp.entries()) {
		if (now - entry.lastAttemptMs >= RESET_WINDOW_MS) {
			failedLoginAttemptsByIp.delete(key);
		}
	}
}

export default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {
	const ip = getRequestIP(event, { xForwardedFor: true }) || 'unknown';
	const now = Date.now();
	pruneExpiredLoginAttempts(now);

	const existingRecord = failedLoginAttemptsByIp.get(ip);
	if (existingRecord && now - existingRecord.lastAttemptMs >= RESET_WINDOW_MS) {
		failedLoginAttemptsByIp.delete(ip);
	}

	const body = await readBody(event);
	const parsed = LoginInputSchema.safeParse(body);
	if (!parsed.success) {
		throw createError({
			statusCode: 400,
			statusMessage: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? 'رمز عبور الزامی است'
		});
	}

	// Honeypot check: reject bots that fill the hidden field
	if (parsed.data.website && parsed.data.website.trim().length > 0) {
		await sleep(3000);
		throw createError({
			statusCode: 401,
			statusMessage: 'رمز عبور وارد شده نادرست است'
		});
	}

	const previousFailures = failedLoginAttemptsByIp.get(ip)?.failedCount ?? 0;
	const currentAttemptNumber = previousFailures + 1;
	const requiredDelayMs = resolveProgressiveDelayMs(currentAttemptNumber);

	const startedAt = Date.now();
	const isValid = await verifyPassword(parsed.data.password);
	const elapsedMs = Date.now() - startedAt;

	if (requiredDelayMs > elapsedMs) {
		await sleep(requiredDelayMs - elapsedMs);
	}

	if (!isValid) {
		const updatedFailures = previousFailures + 1;
		failedLoginAttemptsByIp.set(ip, {
			failedCount: updatedFailures,
			lastAttemptMs: Date.now()
		});

		throw createError({
			statusCode: 401,
			statusMessage: 'رمز عبور وارد شده نادرست است',
			data: {
				failedAttempts: updatedFailures,
				nextDelaySeconds: resolveProgressiveDelayMs(updatedFailures + 1) / 1000
			}
		});
	}

	failedLoginAttemptsByIp.delete(ip);
	const session = await createAuthSession();
	const isSecure = process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production';

	setCookie(event, SESSION_COOKIE_NAME, session.token, getSessionCookieOptions(isSecure));

	return { success: true };
});

================================================================
PATH: server/api/auth/setup.post.ts
================================================================

import { defineEventHandler, readBody, setCookie, createError } from 'h3';
import { SetupPasswordInputSchema } from '~/domain/schemas';
import {
	isPasswordConfigured,
	setupInitialPassword,
	createAuthSession,
	getSessionCookieOptions,
	SESSION_COOKIE_NAME
} from '../../repositories/auth.repo';

export default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {
	if (await isPasswordConfigured()) {
		throw createError({ statusCode: 400, statusMessage: 'رمز عبور قبلاً تنظیم شده است' });
	}

	const body = await readBody(event);
	const parsed = SetupPasswordInputSchema.safeParse(body);
	if (!parsed.success) {
		throw createError({
			statusCode: 400,
			statusMessage: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? 'ورودی نامعتبر است'
		});
	}

	if (parsed.data.website && parsed.data.website.trim().length > 0) {
		await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 2000));
		throw createError({ statusCode: 400, statusMessage: 'درخواست نامعتبر است' });
	}

	await setupInitialPassword(parsed.data.password);
	const session = await createAuthSession();
	const isSecure = process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production';

	setCookie(event, SESSION_COOKIE_NAME, session.token, getSessionCookieOptions(isSecure));

	return { success: true };
});

================================================================
PATH: server/api/auth/change-password.post.ts
================================================================

import { defineEventHandler, readBody, setCookie, createError } from 'h3';
import { ChangePasswordInputSchema } from '~/domain/schemas';
import {
	changeMasterPassword,
	getSessionCookieOptions,
	SESSION_COOKIE_NAME
} from '../../repositories/auth.repo';

export default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {
	const body = await readBody(event);
	const parsed = ChangePasswordInputSchema.safeParse(body);
	if (!parsed.success) {
		throw createError({
			statusCode: 400,
			statusMessage: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? 'اطلاعات وارد شده نامعتبر است'
		});
	}

	const currentToken = (event.context.sessionToken as string | null) ?? null;
	await changeMasterPassword(parsed.data.newPassword, currentToken);

	if (currentToken) {
		const isSecure = process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production';
		setCookie(event, SESSION_COOKIE_NAME, currentToken, getSessionCookieOptions(isSecure));
	}

	return { success: true };
});


----------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================
PATH: server/api/auth/status.get.ts
================================================================

import { defineEventHandler, getCookie, setCookie, deleteCookie } from 'h3';
import {
	isPasswordConfigured,
	validateAndExtendSession,
	getSessionCookieOptions,
	SESSION_COOKIE_NAME
} from '../../repositories/auth.repo';

export default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {
	const configured = await isPasswordConfigured();
	const token = getCookie(event, SESSION_COOKIE_NAME);
	let isAuthenticated = false;
	const isSecure = process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production';

	if (configured && token) {
		const status = await validateAndExtendSession(token, true);
		isAuthenticated = status.valid;

		if (status.valid) {
			setCookie(event, SESSION_COOKIE_NAME, token, getSessionCookieOptions(isSecure));
		} else {
			deleteCookie(event, SESSION_COOKIE_NAME, { path: '/' });
		}
	}

	return {
		isPasswordConfigured: configured,
		isAuthenticated
	};
});

================================================================
PATH: .github/workflows/ci.yml
================================================================

name: CI & Docker Verification

on:
  push:
    branches:
      - main
      - master
  pull_request:
    branches:
      - main
      - master

jobs:
  verify-and-build:
    name: Typecheck, Build & Docker Image Test
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - name: Checkout repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Install pnpm
        uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4
        with:
          run_install: false

      - name: Setup Node.js 22
        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: 22
          cache: 'pnpm'

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: pnpm install

      - name: Run TypeScript typecheck
        run: pnpm run typecheck

      - name: Build Nuxt production bundle
        run: pnpm run build

      - name: Set up Docker Buildx
        uses: docker/setup-buildx-action@v3

      - name: Build Docker image
        uses: docker/build-push-action@v6
        with:
          context: .
          push: false
          load: true
          tags: personal-os-nuxt:ci
          cache-from: type=gha
          cache-to: type=gha,mode=max


----------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================
PATH: nuxt.config.ts
================================================================

import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite';

// https://nuxt.com/docs/api/configuration/nuxt-config
export default defineNuxtConfig({
  ssr: false,

  modules: ['@vite-pwa/nuxt'],

<script setup lang="ts">
import { onMounted } from 'vue';

onMounted(() => {
	if (!import.meta.client || !('serviceWorker' in navigator)) return;

	const checkForSwUpdate = async () => {
		if (!navigator.onLine) return;
		try {
			const registration = await navigator.serviceWorker.getRegistration();
			if (registration) {
				await registration.update();
			}
		} catch {
			// Ignore background update check errors when offline or unreachable
		}
	};

	void checkForSwUpdate();

	document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
		if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') {
			void checkForSwUpdate();
		}
	});
});
</script>

<template>
	<NuxtPwaManifest />
	<NuxtLayout>
		<NuxtPage />
	</NuxtLayout>
</template>

import { COMPOSITE_FULL_LABEL, DEFAULT_HABIT_CATEGORY, TIER_SCORES } from '~/domain/constants';
import type {
	CreateHabitInput,
	DayStatusColor,
	HabitEntity,
	HabitHistoricalSpec,
	HabitLogItemInput,
	HabitRuleDiffItem,
	HabitTreeNode,
	LifecycleEventEntity,
	MutateHabitStructureInput,
	SaveDailyEntryInput
} from '~/domain/schemas';

export function isDailyEntryEmpty(
	input: Pick<SaveDailyEntryInput, 'reflectionNote' | 'journalText' | 'tags' | 'logs'>
): boolean {
	return (
		(!input.reflectionNote || input.reflectionNote.trim() === '') &&
		(!input.journalText || input.journalText.trim() === '') &&
		input.tags.length === 0 &&
		input.logs.every(
			(l) =>
				(l.achievedTier === null || l.achievedTier === undefined) &&
				(l.totalActualMinutes === null ||
					l.totalActualMinutes === 0 ||
					l.totalActualMinutes === undefined)
		)
	);
}

export function resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes(
	log:
		| Partial<
				Pick<
					HabitLogItemInput,
					'actualMinutesPerInterval' | 'totalActualMinutes' | 'completedIntervals'
				>
		  >
		| null
		| undefined
): number {
	if (!log) return 0;
	const perInterval = log.actualMinutesPerInterval ?? 0;
	const intervals = log.completedIntervals;
	if (intervals !== null && intervals !== undefined && intervals > 0 && perInterval > 0) {
		return Number((intervals * perInterval).toFixed(1));
	}
	if (log.totalActualMinutes !== null && log.totalActualMinutes !== undefined && log.totalActualMinutes > 0) {
		return log.totalActualMinutes;
	}
	return perInterval;
}

export function serializeHabitSpecSnapshot(habit: HabitEntity): string {
	const spec: HabitHistoricalSpec = {
		title: habit.title,
		category: habit.category,
		scheduleLabel: habit.scheduleLabel,
		notes: habit.notes,
		bronzeDesc: habit.bronzeDesc,
		silverDesc: habit.silverDesc,
		goldDesc: habit.goldDesc,
		emergencyMinDesc: habit.emergencyMinDesc,
		isSituational: habit.isSituational
	};
	return JSON.stringify(spec);
}

export function parseHabitSpecSnapshot(
	rawJson: string | null | undefined,
	fallbackHabit: HabitEntity | undefined,
	fallbackTitle: string
): HabitHistoricalSpec {
	let parsed: Partial<HabitHistoricalSpec> = {};
	if (rawJson) {
		try {
			parsed = JSON.parse(rawJson) as Partial<HabitHistoricalSpec>;
		} catch {
			parsed = {};
		}
	}

	return {
		title: parsed.title || fallbackTitle,
		category: parsed.category || fallbackHabit?.category || 'عمومی',
		scheduleLabel: parsed.scheduleLabel ?? fallbackHabit?.scheduleLabel ?? null,
		notes: parsed.notes ?? fallbackHabit?.notes ?? null,
		bronzeDesc: parsed.bronzeDesc ?? fallbackHabit?.bronzeDesc ?? null,
		silverDesc: parsed.silverDesc ?? fallbackHabit?.silverDesc ?? null,
		goldDesc: parsed.goldDesc ?? fallbackHabit?.goldDesc ?? null,
		emergencyMinDesc: parsed.emergencyMinDesc ?? fallbackHabit?.emergencyMinDesc ?? null,
		isSituational: Boolean(parsed.isSituational ?? fallbackHabit?.isSituational)
	};
}

export function resolveHabitRuleSnapshot(
	habit: Pick<
		HabitEntity,
		| 'trackingMode'
		| 'scheduleLabel'
		| 'bronzeDesc'
		| 'silverDesc'
		| 'goldDesc'
		| 'emergencyMinDesc'
	>,
	achievedTier: number | null | undefined
): string | null {
	if (habit.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence') {
		return habit.scheduleLabel?.trim() || null;
	}
	if (achievedTier === null || achievedTier === undefined) {
		return null;
	}
	if (achievedTier === TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED) {
		return 'امروز موقعیتش پیش نیامد';
	}
	if (achievedTier === TIER_SCORES.FAILED) {
		const minRule = habit.bronzeDesc?.trim();
		return minRule ? `عدم انجام (${minRule})` : 'انجام نشد';
	}
	if (achievedTier === TIER_SCORES.GOLD) {
		return (
			habit.goldDesc?.trim() ||
			habit.silverDesc?.trim() ||
			habit.bronzeDesc?.trim() ||
			null
		);
	}
	if (achievedTier === TIER_SCORES.SILVER) {
		return habit.silverDesc?.trim() || habit.bronzeDesc?.trim() || null;
	}
	if (achievedTier === TIER_SCORES.BRONZE) {
		return habit.bronzeDesc?.trim() || habit.emergencyMinDesc?.trim() || null;
	}
	return null;
}

export function getApplicableHabitsForDate(
	habits: HabitEntity[],
	options?: {
		dateIso?: string;
		hasExistingEntry?: boolean;
		loggedHabitIds?: ReadonlySet<string>;
	}
): HabitEntity[] {
	const loggedSet = options?.loggedHabitIds ?? new Set<string>();
	const dateIso = options?.dateIso;
	const hasExistingEntry = Boolean(options?.hasExistingEntry);

	const parentIdsWithLoggedChildren = new Set<string>();
	for (const h of habits) {
		if (h.parentId && loggedSet.has(h.id)) {
			parentIdsWithLoggedChildren.add(h.parentId);
		}
	}

	return habits.filter((h) => {
		if (loggedSet.has(h.id) || parentIdsWithLoggedChildren.has(h.id)) {
			return true;
		}
		if (h.status === 'archived') {
			return false;
		}
		if (hasExistingEntry && dateIso && h.createdAtIso.slice(0, 10) > dateIso) {
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	});
}

export function buildHabitTree(
	habits: HabitEntity[],
	options?: {
		dateIso?: string;
		hasExistingEntry?: boolean;
		loggedHabitIds?: ReadonlySet<string>;
	}
): {
	tieredRoots: HabitTreeNode[];
	cadenceHabits: HabitEntity[];
} {
	const applicableHabits = getApplicableHabitsForDate(habits, options);
	const childrenByParent = new Map<string, HabitEntity[]>();

	for (const h of applicableHabits) {
		if (h.parentId) {
			const list = childrenByParent.get(h.parentId) ?? [];
			list.push(h);
			childrenByParent.set(h.parentId, list);
		}
	}

	const tieredRoots: HabitTreeNode[] = [];
	const cadenceHabits: HabitEntity[] = [];

	for (const h of applicableHabits) {
		if (h.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence') {
			cadenceHabits.push(h);
		} else if (!h.parentId) {
			const children = (childrenByParent.get(h.id) ?? []).sort(
				(a, b) => a.sortOrder - b.sortOrder
			);
			tieredRoots.push({
				...h,
				children,
				timeSlot: h.timeSlot
			});
		}
	}

	tieredRoots.sort((a, b) => a.sortOrder - b.sortOrder);
	cadenceHabits.sort((a, b) => a.sortOrder - b.sortOrder);

	return { tieredRoots, cadenceHabits };
}

export function getMaxSupportedTier(
	habit: Pick<HabitEntity, 'bronzeDesc' | 'silverDesc' | 'goldDesc'>
): 1 | 2 | 3 {
	if (habit.goldDesc && habit.goldDesc.trim() !== '' && habit.goldDesc.trim() !== '~') {
		return TIER_SCORES.GOLD;
	}
	if (habit.silverDesc && habit.silverDesc.trim() !== '' && habit.silverDesc.trim() !== '~') {
		return TIER_SCORES.SILVER;
	}
	return TIER_SCORES.BRONZE;
}

export function computeCompositeParentDisplay(
	children: HabitEntity[],
	logsByHabitId: Record<string, HabitLogItemInput>
): string {
	if (children.length === 0) return '-';
	const tokens = children.map((child) => {
		const log = logsByHabitId[child.id];
		if (!log || log.achievedTier === null || log.achievedTier === undefined) {
			return '0';
		}
		if (log.achievedTier === TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED) {
			return '-';
		}
		return String(log.achievedTier);
	});
	const allMax = tokens.every((t) => t === String(TIER_SCORES.GOLD));
	if (allMax) return COMPOSITE_FULL_LABEL;
	return tokens.join('/');
}

export function isHabitNodeCompleted(params: {
	habitId: string;
	habits: HabitEntity[];
	logsByHabitId: Record<string, HabitLogItemInput>;
	hasExistingEntry: boolean;
	isDirty: boolean;
}): boolean {
	const { habitId, habits, logsByHabitId, hasExistingEntry, isDirty } = params;
	const habit = habits.find((h) => h.id === habitId);
	if (!habit) return false;

	const children = habits.filter((h) => h.parentId === habitId && h.status === 'active');
	if (children.length > 0) {
		return children.every((c) => {
			const tier = logsByHabitId[c.id]?.achievedTier;
			return tier !== null && tier !== undefined;
		});
	}

	if (habit.trackingMode === 'tiered') {
		const tier = logsByHabitId[habit.id]?.achievedTier;
		return tier !== null && tier !== undefined;
	}

	const actual = resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes(logsByHabitId[habit.id]);
	return actual > 0 && (hasExistingEntry || isDirty);
}

export function computeDailyCompletionStats(params: {
	applicableHabits: HabitEntity[];
	loggedHabitIds: ReadonlySet<string>;
	logsByHabitId: Record<string, HabitLogItemInput>;
	hasExistingEntry: boolean;
	isDirty: boolean;
}): {
	completed: number;
	goldCount: number;
	total: number;
	percent: number;
} {
	const { applicableHabits, loggedHabitIds, logsByHabitId, hasExistingEntry, isDirty } =
		params;

	const trackable = applicableHabits.filter((h) => {
		if (h.status === 'paused' && !loggedHabitIds.has(h.id)) return false;
		const hasChildren = applicableHabits.some((c) => c.parentId === h.id);
		return !hasChildren;
	});

	const total = trackable.length;
	if (total === 0) return { completed: 0, goldCount: 0, total: 0, percent: 100 };

	let completed = 0;
	let goldCount = 0;

	for (const h of trackable) {
		const log = logsByHabitId[h.id];
		if (!log) continue;

		if (h.trackingMode === 'tiered') {
			const tier = log.achievedTier;
			if (tier === TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED) {
				completed++;
			} else if (tier !== null && tier !== undefined && tier > 0) {
				completed++;
				const maxTier = getMaxSupportedTier(h);
				if (tier === maxTier) goldCount++;
			}
		} else {
			const actual = resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes(log);
			const planned = log.plannedMinutesSnapshot ?? 0;
			if (actual > 0 && (hasExistingEntry || isDirty)) {
				completed++;
				if (planned <= 0 || actual >= planned) goldCount++;
			}
		}
	}

	return {
		completed,
		goldCount,
		total,
		percent: Math.round((completed / total) * 100)
	};
}

export function evaluateDayStatusColor(
	habits: HabitEntity[],
	logs: HabitLogItemInput[]
): DayStatusColor {
	if (logs.length === 0) return 'failed';

	const logMap = new Map(logs.map((l) => [l.habitId, l]));
	const loggedIds = new Set(
		logs
			.filter(
				(l) =>
					(l.achievedTier !== null && l.achievedTier !== undefined) ||
					l.actualMinutesPerInterval !== null ||
					resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes(l) > 0
			)
			.map((l) => l.habitId)
	);
	const trackable = habits.filter((h) => {
		const isLogged = loggedIds.has(h.id);
		if (h.status !== 'active' && !isLogged) return false;
		const hasChildren = habits.some(
			(child) =>
				child.parentId === h.id &&
				(child.status === 'active' || loggedIds.has(child.id))
		);
		return !hasChildren;
	});

	if (trackable.length === 0) return 'optimal';

	let optimalCount = 0;
	let zeroCount = 0;

	for (const habit of trackable) {
		const log = logMap.get(habit.id);
		if (!log) {
			zeroCount++;
			continue;
		}

		if (habit.trackingMode === 'tiered') {
			const tier = log.achievedTier ?? 0;
			if (tier === TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED) {
				optimalCount++;
			} else if (tier === TIER_SCORES.FAILED) {
				zeroCount++;
			} else {
				optimalCount++;
			}
		} else {
			const actualMinutes = resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes(log);
			const plannedMinutes = log.plannedMinutesSnapshot ?? habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval;
			if (actualMinutes <= 0) {
				zeroCount++;
			} else if (plannedMinutes <= 0 || actualMinutes >= plannedMinutes) {
				optimalCount++;
			}
		}
	}

	if (zeroCount === trackable.length) return 'failed';
	if (optimalCount === trackable.length && zeroCount === 0) return 'optimal';
	return 'partial';
}

export function extractLifecycleRuleDiffs(
	prevJson: string | null,
	newJson: string | null
): HabitRuleDiffItem[] {
	if (!prevJson || !newJson) return [];
	try {
		const prev = JSON.parse(prevJson) as Record<string, unknown>;
		const next = JSON.parse(newJson) as Record<string, unknown>;
		const diffs: HabitRuleDiffItem[] = [];

		const fields: Array<{ key: string; label: string }> = [
			{ key: 'title', label: 'عنوان طرح' },
			{ key: 'scheduleLabel', label: 'زمان انجام' },
			{ key: 'bronzeDesc', label: 'شرط برنز (حداقل)' },
			{ key: 'silverDesc', label: 'شرط نقره' },
			{ key: 'goldDesc', label: 'شرط طلا' },
			{ key: 'emergencyMinDesc', label: 'حداقل اضطراری' },
			{ key: 'defaultMinutesPerInterval', label: 'دقیقه هدف در هر بازه' },
			{ key: 'cadenceWindowMinutes', label: 'پنجره زمانی تکرار (دقیقه)' },
			{ key: 'status', label: 'وضعیت فعال‌بودن' },
			{ key: 'notes', label: 'تبصره طرح' }
		];

		for (const f of fields) {
			const oldVal = String(prev[f.key] ?? '').trim();
			const newVal = String(next[f.key] ?? '').trim();
			if (oldVal !== newVal && (oldVal !== '' || newVal !== '')) {
				diffs.push({
					fieldLabel: f.label,
					previousValue: oldVal || 'تعریف‌نشده',
					newValue: newVal || 'حذف‌شده'
				});
			}
		}

		if (
			typeof prev.minutes === 'number' &&
			typeof next.minutes === 'number' &&
			prev.minutes !== next.minutes
		) {
			diffs.push({
				fieldLabel: 'هدف روزانه (دقیقه)',
				previousValue: `${prev.minutes} دقیقه`,
				newValue: `${next.minutes} دقیقه`
			});
		}

		return diffs;
	} catch {
		return [];
	}
}

export function buildCreatedHabitWithLifecycle(params: {
	input: CreateHabitInput;
	maxSortOrder: number;
	todayIso: string;
	nowIso: string;
}): {
	habit: HabitEntity;
	lifecycleEvent: LifecycleEventEntity;
} {
	const { input, maxSortOrder, todayIso, nowIso } = params;

	const habit: HabitEntity = {
		id: crypto.randomUUID(),
		parentId: input.parentId ?? null,
		title: input.title.trim(),
		category: input.category.trim() || DEFAULT_HABIT_CATEGORY,
		trackingMode: input.trackingMode,
		timeSlot: input.timeSlot ?? 'day',
		isSituational: input.isSituational ?? false,
		status: 'active',
		scheduleLabel: input.scheduleLabel?.trim() || null,
		notes: input.notes?.trim() || null,
		bronzeDesc: input.bronzeDesc?.trim() || null,
		silverDesc: input.silverDesc?.trim() || null,
		goldDesc: input.goldDesc?.trim() || null,
		emergencyMinDesc: input.emergencyMinDesc?.trim() || null,
		defaultIntervals: input.defaultIntervals,
		defaultMinutesPerInterval: input.defaultMinutesPerInterval,
		cadenceWindowMinutes: input.cadenceWindowMinutes,
		sortOrder: maxSortOrder + 1,
		createdAtIso: todayIso,
		updatedAtIso: nowIso
	};

	const lifecycleEvent: LifecycleEventEntity = {
		id: crypto.randomUUID(),
		habitId: habit.id,
		habitTitleSnapshot: habit.title,
		eventType: habit.parentId ? 'CONVERTED_TO_SUBHABIT' : 'CREATED',
		effectiveDateIso: todayIso,
		summary: habit.parentId
			? `ایجاد زیرطرح «${habit.title}»`
			: `ایجاد طرح «${habit.title}»`,
		reason: input.reason?.trim() || null,
		previousStateJson: null,
		newStateJson: JSON.stringify(habit),
		createdAtIso: nowIso
	};

	return { habit, lifecycleEvent };
}

export function buildUpdatedHabitWithLifecycle(params: {
	current: HabitEntity;
	input: MutateHabitStructureInput;
	parentTitle?: string | null;
	todayIso: string;
	nowIso: string;
}): {
	updatedHabit: HabitEntity;
	lifecycleEvent: LifecycleEventEntity;
} {
	const { current, input, parentTitle, todayIso, nowIso } = params;

	let eventType: LifecycleEventEntity['eventType'] = 'UPDATED_RULES';
	let summary = `ویرایش شرایط طرح «${input.title}»`;

	if (current.parentId !== input.parentId) {
		if (input.parentId) {
			eventType = 'CONVERTED_TO_SUBHABIT';
			summary = `تبدیل «${input.title}» به زیرطرحِ «${parentTitle ?? 'والد'}»`;
		} else {
			eventType = 'PROMOTED_TO_ROOT';
			summary = `تبدیل «${input.title}» به طرح مستقل`;
		}
	} else if (current.status !== input.status) {
		eventType = input.status === 'archived' ? 'DELETED' : 'STATUS_CHANGED';
		summary = `تغییر وضعیت «${input.title}» به ${input.status}`;
	}

	const updatedHabit: HabitEntity = {
		...current,
		title: input.title.trim(),
		category: input.category.trim() || current.category,
		parentId: input.parentId,
		timeSlot: input.timeSlot ?? current.timeSlot,
		isSituational: input.isSituational,
		status: input.status,
		scheduleLabel: input.scheduleLabel?.trim() || null,
		notes: input.notes?.trim() || null,
		bronzeDesc: input.bronzeDesc?.trim() || null,
		silverDesc: input.silverDesc?.trim() || null,
		goldDesc: input.goldDesc?.trim() || null,
		emergencyMinDesc: input.emergencyMinDesc?.trim() || null,
		updatedAtIso: nowIso
	};

	const lifecycleEvent: LifecycleEventEntity = {
		id: crypto.randomUUID(),
		habitId: input.habitId,
		habitTitleSnapshot: updatedHabit.title,
		eventType,
		effectiveDateIso: todayIso,
		summary,
		reason: input.reason,
		previousStateJson: JSON.stringify(current),
		newStateJson: JSON.stringify(input),
		createdAtIso: nowIso
	};

	return { updatedHabit, lifecycleEvent };
}

================================================================
PATH: app/core/backup-merger.ts
