import type {
	BookEntity,
	CadencePlanEntity,
	CalendarEventEntity,
	DatabaseBackupPayload,
	HabitEntity,
	LifecycleEventEntity,
	MediaEntity,
	NoteEntity
} from '~/domain/schemas';

export type BackupDailyEntryRow = DatabaseBackupPayload['data']['dailyEntries'][number];
export type BackupHabitLogRow = DatabaseBackupPayload['data']['habitLogs'][number];
export type BackupReflectionTagRow = DatabaseBackupPayload['data']['reflectionTags'][number];

export interface ExistingWorkspaceData {
	habits: HabitEntity[];
	dailyEntries: BackupDailyEntryRow[];
	habitLogs: BackupHabitLogRow[];
	cadencePlans: CadencePlanEntity[];
	books: BookEntity[];
	media: MediaEntity[];
	notes: NoteEntity[];
	calendarEvents: CalendarEventEntity[];
	lifecycleEvents: LifecycleEventEntity[];
	reflectionTags: BackupReflectionTagRow[];
}

export interface MergedBackupResult {
	habitsToUpsert: HabitEntity[];
	dailyEntriesToUpsert: BackupDailyEntryRow[];
	habitLogsToUpsert: BackupHabitLogRow[];
	cadencePlansToUpsert: CadencePlanEntity[];
	booksToUpsert: BookEntity[];
	mediaToUpsert: MediaEntity[];
	notesToUpsert: NoteEntity[];
	calendarEventsToUpsert: CalendarEventEntity[];
	lifecycleEventsToUpsert: LifecycleEventEntity[];
	reflectionTagsToUpsert: BackupReflectionTagRow[];
	addedOrUpdatedCount: number;
	skippedDuplicatesCount: number;
}

function normText(str: string | null | undefined): string {
	return (str ?? '').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
}

function isIncomingNewer(
	incomingIso: string | null | undefined,
	existingIso: string | null | undefined
): boolean {
	if (!incomingIso) return false;
	if (!existingIso) return true;
	return incomingIso > existingIso;
}

export function computeSmartBackupMerge(
	existing: ExistingWorkspaceData,
	incoming: DatabaseBackupPayload['data']
): MergedBackupResult {
	let skippedDuplicatesCount = 0;

	// 1. Habits deduplication (by ID or by normalized Title + TrackingMode) & ID Remapping
	const habitIdRemap = new Map<string, string>();
	const existingHabitById = new Map(existing.habits.map((h) => [h.id, h]));
	const existingHabitBySignature = new Map<string, HabitEntity>();

	for (const h of existing.habits) {
		const parentTitle = h.parentId ? existingHabitById.get(h.parentId)?.title : '';
		const sig = `${normText(h.title)}|${h.trackingMode}|${normText(parentTitle)}`;
		existingHabitBySignature.set(sig, h);
	}

	const incomingHabitById = new Map(incoming.habits.map((h) => [h.id, h]));
	const habitsToUpsert: HabitEntity[] = [];

	// First pass: map IDs
	for (const incHabit of incoming.habits) {
		const byId = existingHabitById.get(incHabit.id);
		if (byId) {
			habitIdRemap.set(incHabit.id, byId.id);
			continue;
		}
		const incParentTitle = incHabit.parentId
			? incomingHabitById.get(incHabit.parentId)?.title
			: '';
		const sig = `${normText(incHabit.title)}|${incHabit.trackingMode}|${normText(incParentTitle)}`;
		const bySig = existingHabitBySignature.get(sig);
		if (bySig) {
			habitIdRemap.set(incHabit.id, bySig.id);
		} else {
			habitIdRemap.set(incHabit.id, incHabit.id);
		}
	}

	// Second pass: decide which habits to insert/update
	for (const incHabit of incoming.habits) {
		const targetId = habitIdRemap.get(incHabit.id) ?? incHabit.id;
		const remappedParentId = incHabit.parentId
			? (habitIdRemap.get(incHabit.parentId) ?? incHabit.parentId)
			: null;

		const normalizedIncoming: HabitEntity = {
			...incHabit,
			id: targetId,
			parentId: remappedParentId
		};

		const existingMatch = existingHabitById.get(targetId);
		if (!existingMatch) {
			habitsToUpsert.push(normalizedIncoming);
			existingHabitById.set(targetId, normalizedIncoming);
		} else if (isIncomingNewer(incHabit.updatedAtIso, existingMatch.updatedAtIso)) {
			habitsToUpsert.push(normalizedIncoming);
			existingHabitById.set(targetId, normalizedIncoming);
		} else {
			skippedDuplicatesCount++;
		}
	}

	// 2. Daily Entries (Natural key: dateIso)
	const existingEntryByDate = new Map(existing.dailyEntries.map((e) => [e.dateIso, e]));
	const dailyEntriesToUpsert: BackupDailyEntryRow[] = [];

	for (const incEntry of incoming.dailyEntries) {
		const ext = existingEntryByDate.get(incEntry.dateIso);
		if (!ext) {
			dailyEntriesToUpsert.push({
				...incEntry,
				id: `entry:${incEntry.dateIso}`
			});
		} else {
			const sameContent =
				ext.dayStatus === incEntry.dayStatus &&
				normText(ext.reflectionNote) === normText(incEntry.reflectionNote) &&
				normText(ext.journalText) === normText(incEntry.journalText) &&
				ext.tagsJson === incEntry.tagsJson;

			if (sameContent || !isIncomingNewer(incEntry.updatedAtIso, ext.updatedAtIso)) {
				skippedDuplicatesCount++;
			} else {
				dailyEntriesToUpsert.push({
					...incEntry,
					id: ext.id || `entry:${incEntry.dateIso}`
				});
			}
		}
	}

	// 3. Habit Logs (Natural key: dateIso + remapped habitId)
	const existingLogByKey = new Map(
		existing.habitLogs.map((l) => [`${l.dateIso}|${l.habitId}`, l])
	);
	const habitLogsToUpsert: BackupHabitLogRow[] = [];

	for (const incLog of incoming.habitLogs) {
		const remappedHabitId = habitIdRemap.get(incLog.habitId) ?? incLog.habitId;
		const key = `${incLog.dateIso}|${remappedHabitId}`;
		const ext = existingLogByKey.get(key);

		const normalizedLog: BackupHabitLogRow = {
			...incLog,
			id: ext?.id || `log:${incLog.dateIso}:${remappedHabitId}`,
			habitId: remappedHabitId
		};

		if (!ext) {
			habitLogsToUpsert.push(normalizedLog);
			existingLogByKey.set(key, normalizedLog);
		} else {
			const isIdentical =
				ext.achievedTier === normalizedLog.achievedTier &&
				(ext.actualMinutesPerInterval ?? null) ===
					(normalizedLog.actualMinutesPerInterval ?? null) &&
				(ext.totalActualMinutes ?? null) === (normalizedLog.totalActualMinutes ?? null);

			if (isIdentical || !isIncomingNewer(normalizedLog.updatedAtIso, ext.updatedAtIso)) {
				skippedDuplicatesCount++;
			} else {
				habitLogsToUpsert.push(normalizedLog);
				existingLogByKey.set(key, normalizedLog);
			}
		}
	}

	// 4. Cadence Plans (Natural key: dateIso + remapped habitId)
	const existingPlanByKey = new Map(
		existing.cadencePlans.map((p) => [`${p.dateIso}|${p.habitId}`, p])
	);
	const cadencePlansToUpsert: CadencePlanEntity[] = [];

	for (const incPlan of incoming.cadencePlans) {
		const remappedHabitId = habitIdRemap.get(incPlan.habitId) ?? incPlan.habitId;
		const key = `${incPlan.dateIso}|${remappedHabitId}`;
		const ext = existingPlanByKey.get(key);

		const normalizedPlan: CadencePlanEntity = {
			...incPlan,
			id: ext?.id || `plan:${incPlan.dateIso}:${remappedHabitId}`,
			habitId: remappedHabitId
		};

		if (!ext) {
			cadencePlansToUpsert.push(normalizedPlan);
			existingPlanByKey.set(key, normalizedPlan);
		} else {
			const isIdentical =
				ext.plannedIntervals === normalizedPlan.plannedIntervals &&
				ext.plannedMinutesPerInterval === normalizedPlan.plannedMinutesPerInterval &&
				ext.cadenceWindowMinutes === normalizedPlan.cadenceWindowMinutes;

			if (isIdentical || !isIncomingNewer(normalizedPlan.updatedAtIso, ext.updatedAtIso)) {
				skippedDuplicatesCount++;
			} else {
				cadencePlansToUpsert.push(normalizedPlan);
				existingPlanByKey.set(key, normalizedPlan);
			}
		}
	}

	// 5. Books (Deduplicate by ID or by normalized Title + Author + FinishedAtIso)
	const existingBookById = new Map(existing.books.map((b) => [b.id, b]));
	const existingBookBySig = new Map(
		existing.books.map((b) => [
			`${normText(b.title)}|${normText(b.author)}|${b.finishedAtIso ?? ''}`,
			b
		])
	);
	const booksToUpsert: BookEntity[] = [];

	for (const incBook of incoming.books) {
		const sig = `${normText(incBook.title)}|${normText(incBook.author)}|${incBook.finishedAtIso ?? ''}`;
		const ext = existingBookById.get(incBook.id) ?? existingBookBySig.get(sig);

		if (!ext) {
			booksToUpsert.push(incBook);
			existingBookById.set(incBook.id, incBook);
			existingBookBySig.set(sig, incBook);
		} else {
			const isIdentical =
				normText(ext.title) === normText(incBook.title) &&
				normText(ext.author) === normText(incBook.author) &&
				ext.status === incBook.status &&
				ext.rating === incBook.rating &&
				normText(ext.notes) === normText(incBook.notes);

			if (isIdentical || !isIncomingNewer(incBook.updatedAtIso, ext.updatedAtIso)) {
				skippedDuplicatesCount++;
			} else {
				const mergedBook: BookEntity = { ...incBook, id: ext.id };
				booksToUpsert.push(mergedBook);
				existingBookById.set(ext.id, mergedBook);
				existingBookBySig.set(sig, mergedBook);
			}
		}
	}

	// 6. Media (Deduplicate by ID or by normalized Title + Category + FinishedAtIso)
	const existingMediaById = new Map(existing.media.map((m) => [m.id, m]));
	const existingMediaBySig = new Map(
		existing.media.map((m) => [
			`${normText(m.title)}|${m.category}|${m.finishedAtIso ?? ''}`,
			m
		])
	);
	const mediaToUpsert: MediaEntity[] = [];

	for (const incMedia of incoming.media) {
		const sig = `${normText(incMedia.title)}|${incMedia.category}|${incMedia.finishedAtIso ?? ''}`;
		const ext = existingMediaById.get(incMedia.id) ?? existingMediaBySig.get(sig);

		if (!ext) {
			mediaToUpsert.push(incMedia);
			existingMediaById.set(incMedia.id, incMedia);
			existingMediaBySig.set(sig, incMedia);
		} else {
			const isIdentical =
				normText(ext.title) === normText(incMedia.title) &&
				ext.category === incMedia.category &&
				ext.status === incMedia.status &&
				ext.rating === incMedia.rating &&
				normText(ext.notes) === normText(incMedia.notes);

			if (isIdentical || !isIncomingNewer(incMedia.updatedAtIso, ext.updatedAtIso)) {
				skippedDuplicatesCount++;
			} else {
				const mergedMedia: MediaEntity = { ...incMedia, id: ext.id };
				mediaToUpsert.push(mergedMedia);
				existingMediaById.set(ext.id, mergedMedia);
				existingMediaBySig.set(sig, mergedMedia);
			}
		}
	}

	// 7. Notes (Deduplicate by ID or by normalized Title + Content)
	const existingNoteById = new Map(existing.notes.map((n) => [n.id, n]));
	const existingNoteBySig = new Map(
		existing.notes.map((n) => [`${normText(n.title)}|${normText(n.content)}`, n])
	);
	const notesToUpsert: NoteEntity[] = [];

	for (const incNote of incoming.notes) {
		const sig = `${normText(incNote.title)}|${normText(incNote.content)}`;
		const ext = existingNoteById.get(incNote.id) ?? existingNoteBySig.get(sig);

		if (!ext) {
			notesToUpsert.push(incNote);
			existingNoteById.set(incNote.id, incNote);
			existingNoteBySig.set(sig, incNote);
		} else {
			const isIdentical =
				normText(ext.title) === normText(incNote.title) &&
				normText(ext.content) === normText(incNote.content) &&
				normText(ext.category) === normText(incNote.category) &&
				ext.isPinned === incNote.isPinned;

			if (isIdentical || !isIncomingNewer(incNote.updatedAtIso, ext.updatedAtIso)) {
				skippedDuplicatesCount++;
			} else {
				const mergedNote: NoteEntity = { ...incNote, id: ext.id };
				notesToUpsert.push(mergedNote);
				existingNoteById.set(ext.id, mergedNote);
				existingNoteBySig.set(sig, mergedNote);
			}
		}
	}

	// 8. Calendar Events (Deduplicate by ID or by Title + Annual + Date/Jalali)
	const existingEventById = new Map(existing.calendarEvents.map((e) => [e.id, e]));
	const existingEventBySig = new Map(
		existing.calendarEvents.map((e) => [
			`${normText(e.title)}|${e.isAnnual ? '1' : '0'}|${e.dateIso ?? ''}|${e.jalaliMonth}-${e.jalaliDay}`,
			e
		])
	);
	const calendarEventsToUpsert: CalendarEventEntity[] = [];

	for (const incEv of incoming.calendarEvents) {
		const sig = `${normText(incEv.title)}|${incEv.isAnnual ? '1' : '0'}|${incEv.dateIso ?? ''}|${incEv.jalaliMonth}-${incEv.jalaliDay}`;
		const ext = existingEventById.get(incEv.id) ?? existingEventBySig.get(sig);

		if (!ext) {
			calendarEventsToUpsert.push(incEv);
			existingEventById.set(incEv.id, incEv);
			existingEventBySig.set(sig, incEv);
		} else {
			const isIdentical =
				normText(ext.title) === normText(incEv.title) &&
				ext.isCompleted === incEv.isCompleted &&
				normText(ext.notes) === normText(incEv.notes);

			if (isIdentical || !isIncomingNewer(incEv.updatedAtIso, ext.updatedAtIso)) {
				skippedDuplicatesCount++;
			} else {
				const mergedEv: CalendarEventEntity = { ...incEv, id: ext.id };
				calendarEventsToUpsert.push(mergedEv);
				existingEventById.set(ext.id, mergedEv);
				existingEventBySig.set(sig, mergedEv);
			}
		}
	}

	// 9. Lifecycle Events (Deduplicate by ID or by remappedHabitId + eventType + effectiveDateIso + summary)
	const existingLcById = new Set(existing.lifecycleEvents.map((lc) => lc.id));
	const existingLcBySig = new Set(
		existing.lifecycleEvents.map(
			(lc) => `${lc.habitId ?? ''}|${lc.eventType}|${lc.effectiveDateIso}|${normText(lc.summary)}`
		)
	);
	const lifecycleEventsToUpsert: LifecycleEventEntity[] = [];

	for (const incLc of incoming.lifecycleEvents) {
		const remappedHabitId = incLc.habitId
			? (habitIdRemap.get(incLc.habitId) ?? incLc.habitId)
			: null;
		const sig = `${remappedHabitId ?? ''}|${incLc.eventType}|${incLc.effectiveDateIso}|${normText(incLc.summary)}`;

		if (existingLcById.has(incLc.id) || existingLcBySig.has(sig)) {
			skippedDuplicatesCount++;
		} else {
			const normalizedLc: LifecycleEventEntity = {
				...incLc,
				habitId: remappedHabitId
			};
			lifecycleEventsToUpsert.push(normalizedLc);
			existingLcById.add(normalizedLc.id);
			existingLcBySig.add(sig);
		}
	}

	// 10. Reflection Tags (Deduplicate by normalized label)
	const existingTagLabels = new Set(existing.reflectionTags.map((t) => normText(t.label)));
	const reflectionTagsToUpsert: BackupReflectionTagRow[] = [];

	for (const incTag of incoming.reflectionTags) {
		const normLabel = normText(incTag.label);
		if (existingTagLabels.has(normLabel)) {
			skippedDuplicatesCount++;
		} else {
			reflectionTagsToUpsert.push(incTag);
			existingTagLabels.add(normLabel);
		}
	}

	const addedOrUpdatedCount =
		habitsToUpsert.length +
		dailyEntriesToUpsert.length +
		habitLogsToUpsert.length +
		cadencePlansToUpsert.length +
		booksToUpsert.length +
		mediaToUpsert.length +
		notesToUpsert.length +
		calendarEventsToUpsert.length +
		lifecycleEventsToUpsert.length +
		reflectionTagsToUpsert.length;

	return {
		habitsToUpsert,
		dailyEntriesToUpsert,
		habitLogsToUpsert,
		cadencePlansToUpsert,
		booksToUpsert,
		mediaToUpsert,
		notesToUpsert,
		calendarEventsToUpsert,
		lifecycleEventsToUpsert,
		reflectionTagsToUpsert,
		addedOrUpdatedCount,
		skippedDuplicatesCount
	};
}


----------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================
PATH: app/core/note-signals.ts
================================================================

import { normalizeDigits } from '~/domain/calendar';

const LINK_PATTERN =
	/https?:\/\/[^\s]+|www\.[^\s]+|\b[a-zA-Z0-9-]+\.(com|ir|org|net|io|dev|app|me|co|ai|gg|xyz)(\/[^\s]*)?/i;
const TEN_DIGIT_NUMBER_PATTERN = /\d{10,}/;

export function detectNoteSmartSignals(text: string): {
	hasLink: boolean;
	hasNumber: boolean;
	firstUrl: string | null;
} {
	const normalized = normalizeDigits(text);
	const linkMatch = normalized.match(LINK_PATTERN);
	const rawLink = linkMatch ? linkMatch[0] : null;
	const firstUrl = rawLink
		? /^https?:\/\//i.test(rawLink)
			? rawLink
			: `https://${rawLink}`
		: null;

	return {
		hasLink: Boolean(firstUrl),
		hasNumber: TEN_DIGIT_NUMBER_PATTERN.test(normalized),
		firstUrl
	};
}

================================================================
PATH: app/core/analytics-engine.ts

import {
	formatJalaliHuman,
	getDaysUntilIso,
	getPersianWeekdayIndex,
	getTodayIso,
	isoToDate,
	isoToJalaliSlash,
	JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS,
	shiftIsoDate
} from '~/domain/calendar';
import {
	CADENCE_SUCCESS_RATIOS,
	LIFECYCLE_EVENT_META,
	QUALITY_SCORES,
	TIER_SCORES
} from '~/domain/constants';
import type {
	AnalyticsRawEntry,
	AnalyticsRawLog,
	AnalyticsTimeRange,
	DayHabitOutcome,
	FullAnalyticsReport,
	HabitComparisonRow,
	HabitDailyPoint,
	HabitEntity,
	HabitEvolutionItem,
	LifecycleEventEntity,
	SelectedHabitAnalytics,
	TagCorrelationStat,
	WeekdayPerformanceStat
} from '~/domain/schemas';
import {
	extractLifecycleRuleDiffs,
	getMaxSupportedTier,
	resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes,
	resolveHabitRuleSnapshot
} from './habit-engine';

function resolveDateRangeWindow(
	range: AnalyticsTimeRange,
	entries: AnalyticsRawEntry[],
	logs: AnalyticsRawLog[],
	habits: HabitEntity[],
	fallbackEndIso: string
): { startIso: string; endIso: string; dateList: string[] } {
	let maxIso = fallbackEndIso;
	let minIso = fallbackEndIso;

	for (const e of entries) {
		if (e.dateIso > maxIso) maxIso = e.dateIso;
		if (e.dateIso < minIso) minIso = e.dateIso;
	}
	for (const l of logs) {
		if (l.dateIso > maxIso) maxIso = l.dateIso;
		if (l.dateIso < minIso) minIso = l.dateIso;
	}
	for (const h of habits) {
		if (h.createdAtIso && h.createdAtIso < minIso) {
			minIso = h.createdAtIso;
		}
	}

	const endIso = maxIso;
	let startIso = endIso;

	if (range === '7d') {
		startIso = shiftIsoDate(endIso, -6);
	} else if (range === '30d') {
		startIso = shiftIsoDate(endIso, -29);
	} else if (range === '90d') {
		startIso = shiftIsoDate(endIso, -89);
	} else {
		const totalSpan = getDaysUntilIso(minIso, endIso) + 1;
		if (totalSpan < 14) {
			startIso = shiftIsoDate(endIso, -13);
		} else {
			startIso = minIso;
		}
	}

	const daysCount = Math.max(1, getDaysUntilIso(startIso, endIso) + 1);
	const dateList: string[] = [];
	for (let i = 0; i < daysCount; i++) {
		dateList.push(shiftIsoDate(startIso, i));
	}

	return { startIso, endIso, dateList };
}

function evaluateSingleLeafHabitDay(
	habit: HabitEntity,
	log: AnalyticsRawLog | undefined,
	hasDailyEntry: boolean
): {
	outcome: DayHabitOutcome;
	tierLevel: number | null;
	score: number | null;
	ruleSnapshot: string | null;
	actualMinutes: number | null;
	plannedMinutes: number | null;
	completedIntervals: number | null;
} {
	if (habit.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence') {
		const plannedMinutes =
			log?.plannedMinutesSnapshot && log.plannedMinutesSnapshot > 0
				? log.plannedMinutesSnapshot
				: habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval > 0
					? habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval
					: 10;

		if (!log && !hasDailyEntry) {
			return {
				outcome: 'unlogged',
				tierLevel: null,
				score: null,
				ruleSnapshot: null,
				actualMinutes: null,
				plannedMinutes,
				completedIntervals: null
			};
		}

		const actualMinutes = log ? resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes(log) : 0;
		const completedIntervals = log?.completedIntervals ?? null;

		if (actualMinutes <= 0) {
			if (!log) {
				return {
					outcome: 'unlogged',
					tierLevel: null,
					score: null,
					ruleSnapshot: null,
					actualMinutes: null,
					plannedMinutes,
					completedIntervals: null
				};
			}
			return {
				outcome: 'failed',
				tierLevel: 0,
				score: 0,
				ruleSnapshot: `۰ دقیقه از هدف ${plannedMinutes} دقیقه`,
				actualMinutes: 0,
				plannedMinutes,
				completedIntervals
			};
		}

		const ratio = plannedMinutes > 0 ? actualMinutes / plannedMinutes : 1;
		const score = Math.min(100, Math.round(ratio * 100));

		if (ratio >= CADENCE_SUCCESS_RATIOS.GOLD_THRESHOLD) {
			return {
				outcome: 'gold',
				tierLevel: 3,
				score: QUALITY_SCORES.GOLD,
				ruleSnapshot: `${actualMinutes} دقیقه (تکمیل هدف ${plannedMinutes} دقیقه)`,
				actualMinutes,
				plannedMinutes,
				completedIntervals
			};
		}
		if (ratio >= CADENCE_SUCCESS_RATIOS.SILVER_THRESHOLD) {
			return {
				outcome: 'silver',
				tierLevel: 2,
				score,
				ruleSnapshot: `${actualMinutes} دقیقه از هدف ${plannedMinutes} دقیقه`,
				actualMinutes,
				plannedMinutes,
				completedIntervals
			};
		}
		return {
			outcome: 'bronze',
			tierLevel: 1,
			score: Math.max(QUALITY_SCORES.CADENCE_MIN_NONZERO, score),
			ruleSnapshot: `${actualMinutes} دقیقه از هدف ${plannedMinutes} دقیقه`,
			actualMinutes,
			plannedMinutes,
			completedIntervals
		};
	}

	if (!log || log.achievedTier === null || log.achievedTier === undefined) {
		return {
			outcome: 'unlogged',
			tierLevel: null,
			score: null,
			ruleSnapshot: null,
			actualMinutes: null,
			plannedMinutes: null,
			completedIntervals: null
		};
	}

	const tier = log.achievedTier;
	const maxTier = getMaxSupportedTier(habit);
	const ruleSnapshot =
		log.achievedRuleSnapshot || resolveHabitRuleSnapshot(habit, tier) || habit.scheduleLabel;

	if (tier === TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED) {
		return {
			outcome: 'skipped',
			tierLevel: -1,
			score: null,
			ruleSnapshot: ruleSnapshot || 'امروز موقعیتش پیش نیامد',
			actualMinutes: null,
			plannedMinutes: null,
			completedIntervals: null
		};
	}

	if (tier === TIER_SCORES.FAILED) {
		return {
			outcome: 'failed',
			tierLevel: 0,
			score: QUALITY_SCORES.FAILED,
			ruleSnapshot: ruleSnapshot || 'انجام نشد',
			actualMinutes: null,
			plannedMinutes: null,
			completedIntervals: null
		};
	}

	if (tier >= maxTier || tier === TIER_SCORES.GOLD) {
		return {
			outcome: 'gold',
			tierLevel: 3,
			score: QUALITY_SCORES.GOLD,
			ruleSnapshot,
			actualMinutes: null,
			plannedMinutes: null,
			completedIntervals: null
		};
	}

	if (tier === TIER_SCORES.SILVER) {
		return {
			outcome: 'silver',
			tierLevel: 2,
			score: QUALITY_SCORES.SILVER,
			ruleSnapshot,
			actualMinutes: null,
			plannedMinutes: null,
			completedIntervals: null
		};
	}

	return {
		outcome: 'bronze',
		tierLevel: 1,
		score: maxTier === 1 ? QUALITY_SCORES.BRONZE_MAX : QUALITY_SCORES.BRONZE_DEFAULT,
		ruleSnapshot,
		actualMinutes: null,
		plannedMinutes: null,
		completedIntervals: null
	};
}

function evaluateHabitDay(
	habit: HabitEntity,
	children: HabitEntity[],
	logsByHabitOnDate: Map<string, AnalyticsRawLog> | undefined,
	hasDailyEntry: boolean
) {
	if (children.length === 0) {
		const log = logsByHabitOnDate?.get(habit.id);
		return evaluateSingleLeafHabitDay(habit, log, hasDailyEntry);
	}

	const childResults = children.map((child) => ({
		child,
		evalResult: evaluateSingleLeafHabitDay(
			child,
			logsByHabitOnDate?.get(child.id),
			hasDailyEntry
		)
	}));

	const loggedChildren = childResults.filter((r) => r.evalResult.outcome !== 'unlogged');
	if (loggedChildren.length === 0) {
		return {
			outcome: 'unlogged' as DayHabitOutcome,
			tierLevel: null,
			score: null,
			ruleSnapshot: null,
			actualMinutes: null,
			plannedMinutes: null,
			completedIntervals: null
		};
	}

	const scoredChildren = loggedChildren.filter((r) => r.evalResult.score !== null);
	if (scoredChildren.length === 0) {
		return {
			outcome: 'skipped' as DayHabitOutcome,
			tierLevel: -1,
			score: null,
			ruleSnapshot: 'موقعیت زیرطرح‌ها پیش نیامد',
			actualMinutes: null,
			plannedMinutes: null,
			completedIntervals: null
		};
	}

	const avgScore = Math.round(
		scoredChildren.reduce((acc, r) => acc + (r.evalResult.score ?? 0), 0) /
			scoredChildren.length
	);

	let outcome: DayHabitOutcome = 'bronze';
	let tierLevel = 1;
	if (avgScore === 0) {
		outcome = 'failed';
		tierLevel = 0;
	} else if (avgScore >= 88) {
		outcome = 'gold';
		tierLevel = 3;
	} else if (avgScore >= 55) {
		outcome = 'silver';
		tierLevel = 2;
	}

	const summaryTokens = loggedChildren.map((r) => {
		const label =
			r.evalResult.outcome === 'gold'
				? 'طلا'
				: r.evalResult.outcome === 'silver'
					? 'نقره'
					: r.evalResult.outcome === 'bronze'
						? 'برنز'
						: r.evalResult.outcome === 'skipped'
							? 'پیش نیامد'
							: 'نشد';
		return `${r.child.title}: ${label}`;
	});

	return {
		outcome,
		tierLevel,
		score: avgScore,
		ruleSnapshot: summaryTokens.join(' • '),
		actualMinutes: null,
		plannedMinutes: null,
		completedIntervals: null
	};
}

function buildEvolutionTimelineForHabit(
	habit: HabitEntity,
	children: HabitEntity[],
	allEvents: LifecycleEventEntity[],
	allLogsByDate: Map<string, Map<string, AnalyticsRawLog>>,
	entryByDate: Map<string, AnalyticsRawEntry>,
	referenceTodayIso: string
): HabitEvolutionItem[] {
	const relevantIds = new Set<string>([habit.id, ...children.map((c) => c.id)]);
	const relevantEvents = allEvents
		.filter((ev) => ev.habitId && relevantIds.has(ev.habitId))
		.sort((a, b) => (b.createdAtIso > a.createdAtIso ? 1 : b.createdAtIso < a.createdAtIso ? -1 : 0));

	return relevantEvents.map((ev) => {
		const meta = LIFECYCLE_EVENT_META[ev.eventType];
		const human = formatJalaliHuman(ev.effectiveDateIso, referenceTodayIso);
		const diffs = extractLifecycleRuleDiffs(ev.previousStateJson, ev.newStateJson);

		const beforeScores: number[] = [];
		const afterScores: number[] = [];

		for (let offset = -14; offset < 0; offset++) {
			const dIso = shiftIsoDate(ev.effectiveDateIso, offset);
			const res = evaluateHabitDay(
				habit,
				children,
				allLogsByDate.get(dIso),
				entryByDate.has(dIso)
			);
			if (res.score !== null) beforeScores.push(res.score);
		}

		for (let offset = 0; offset <= 14; offset++) {
			const dIso = shiftIsoDate(ev.effectiveDateIso, offset);
			const res = evaluateHabitDay(
				habit,
				children,
				allLogsByDate.get(dIso),
				entryByDate.has(dIso)
			);
			if (res.score !== null) afterScores.push(res.score);
		}

		const beforeSuccessRate =
			beforeScores.length > 0
				? Math.round(beforeScores.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / beforeScores.length)
				: null;

		const afterSuccessRate =
			afterScores.length > 0
				? Math.round(afterScores.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / afterScores.length)
				: null;

		const impactDelta =
			beforeSuccessRate !== null && afterSuccessRate !== null
				? afterSuccessRate - beforeSuccessRate
				: null;

		return {
			id: ev.id,
			habitId: ev.habitId,
			habitTitle: ev.habitTitleSnapshot,
			eventType: ev.eventType,
			eventTypeLabel: meta.label,
			badgeClasses: meta.badgeClasses,
			effectiveDateIso: ev.effectiveDateIso,
			jalaliSlash: isoToJalaliSlash(ev.effectiveDateIso),
			humanDateLabel: human.fullLabel,
			summary: ev.summary,
			reason: ev.reason,
			diffs,
			beforeSuccessRate,
			afterSuccessRate,
			impactDelta
		};
	});
}

function computeStreaksFromSeries(
	series: ReadonlyArray<{ outcome: DayHabitOutcome }>
): {
	currentStreak: number;
	bestStreak: number;
} {
	let bestStreak = 0;
	let running = 0;

	for (const pt of series) {
		if (pt.outcome === 'gold' || pt.outcome === 'silver' || pt.outcome === 'bronze') {
			running++;
			if (running > bestStreak) bestStreak = running;
		} else if (pt.outcome === 'failed') {
			running = 0;
		}
	}

	let currentStreak = 0;
	for (let i = series.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
		const pt = series[i];
		if (!pt) break;
		const out = pt.outcome;
		if (out === 'gold' || out === 'silver' || out === 'bronze') {
			currentStreak++;
		} else if (out === 'failed') {
			break;
		}
	}

	return { currentStreak, bestStreak };
}

function computeTrendDelta(validScores: ReadonlyArray<number>, fallbackAvg: number): number {
	if (validScores.length < 2) return 0;
	const mid = Math.floor(validScores.length / 2);
	const firstHalf = validScores.slice(0, mid);
	const secondHalf = validScores.slice(mid);
	const firstAvg =
		firstHalf.length > 0
			? firstHalf.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / firstHalf.length
			: fallbackAvg;
	const secondAvg =
		secondHalf.length > 0
			? secondHalf.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / secondHalf.length
			: fallbackAvg;
	return Math.round(secondAvg - firstAvg);
}

export function buildFullAnalyticsReport(params: {
	habits: HabitEntity[];
	entries: AnalyticsRawEntry[];
	logs: AnalyticsRawLog[];
	lifecycleEvents: LifecycleEventEntity[];
	selectedHabitId?: string | null;
	range: AnalyticsTimeRange;
	fallbackEndDateIso: string;
}): FullAnalyticsReport {
	const {
		habits,
		entries,
		logs,
		lifecycleEvents,
		selectedHabitId,
		range,
		fallbackEndDateIso
	} = params;

	const habitById = new Map(habits.map((h) => [h.id, h]));
	const childrenByParentId = new Map<string, HabitEntity[]>();
	for (const h of habits) {
		if (h.parentId) {
			const list = childrenByParentId.get(h.parentId) ?? [];
			list.push(h);
			childrenByParentId.set(h.parentId, list);
		}
	}

	const entryByDate = new Map(entries.map((e) => [e.dateIso, e]));
	const logsByDate = new Map<string, Map<string, AnalyticsRawLog>>();
	for (const log of logs) {
		let dayMap = logsByDate.get(log.dateIso);
		if (!dayMap) {
			dayMap = new Map();
			logsByDate.set(log.dateIso, dayMap);
		}
		dayMap.set(log.habitId, log);
	}

	const { startIso, endIso, dateList } = resolveDateRangeWindow(
		range,
		entries,
		logs,
		habits,
		fallbackEndDateIso
	);

	const sortedHabits = [...habits].sort((a, b) => {
		if (a.status === 'archived' && b.status !== 'archived') return 1;
		if (a.status !== 'archived' && b.status === 'archived') return -1;
		return a.sortOrder - b.sortOrder;
	});

	const selectableHabits = sortedHabits.map((h) => {
		const parent = h.parentId ? habitById.get(h.parentId) : null;
		const children = childrenByParentId.get(h.id) ?? [];
		const isComposite = children.length > 0;
		const prefix = parent ? `↳ ${h.title}` : h.title;
		const suffix = h.status === 'archived' ? ' (بایگانی)' : '';

		return {
			habit: h,
			parentTitle: parent?.title ?? null,
			isComposite,
			displayLabel: `${prefix}${suffix}`
		};
	});

	const targetHabit =
		(selectedHabitId ? habitById.get(selectedHabitId) : null) ??
		selectableHabits[0]?.habit ??
		null;

	let optimalDaysCount = 0;
	let partialDaysCount = 0;
	let failedDaysCount = 0;
	for (const dIso of dateList) {
		const entry = entryByDate.get(dIso);
		if (!entry) continue;
		if (entry.dayStatus === 'optimal') optimalDaysCount++;
		else if (entry.dayStatus === 'partial') partialDaysCount++;
		else if (entry.dayStatus === 'failed') failedDaysCount++;
	}
	const totalLoggedDays = optimalDaysCount + partialDaysCount + failedDaysCount;
	const overallConsistencyRate =
		totalLoggedDays > 0
			? Math.round(((optimalDaysCount + partialDaysCount) / totalLoggedDays) * 100)
			: 0;

	const eventsInWindow = lifecycleEvents.filter(
		(e) => e.effectiveDateIso >= startIso && e.effectiveDateIso <= endIso
	);

	const comparisonRows: HabitComparisonRow[] = selectableHabits.map((item) => {
		const h = item.habit;
		const children = childrenByParentId.get(h.id) ?? [];
		const seriesScores: Array<{ iso: string; outcome: DayHabitOutcome; score: number | null }> =
			[];

		let goldCount = 0;
		let silverCount = 0;
		let bronzeCount = 0;
		let failedCount = 0;
		let skippedCount = 0;

		for (const dIso of dateList) {
			const res = evaluateHabitDay(
				h,
				children,
				logsByDate.get(dIso),
				entryByDate.has(dIso)
			);
			seriesScores.push({ iso: dIso, outcome: res.outcome, score: res.score });
			if (res.outcome === 'gold') goldCount++;
			else if (res.outcome === 'silver') silverCount++;
			else if (res.outcome === 'bronze') bronzeCount++;
			else if (res.outcome === 'failed') failedCount++;
			else if (res.outcome === 'skipped') skippedCount++;
		}

		const loggedDays = goldCount + silverCount + bronzeCount + failedCount;
		const successDays = goldCount + silverCount + bronzeCount;
		const consistencyRate =
			loggedDays > 0 ? Math.round((successDays / loggedDays) * 100) : 0;

		const validScores = seriesScores
			.map((s) => s.score)
			.filter((s): s is number => s !== null);
		const avgQualityScore =
			validScores.length > 0
				? Math.round(validScores.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / validScores.length)
				: 0;

		const trendDelta = computeTrendDelta(validScores, avgQualityScore);
		const { currentStreak, bestStreak } = computeStreaksFromSeries(seriesScores);

		const relevantIds = new Set([h.id, ...children.map((c) => c.id)]);
		const lifecycleChangesCount = lifecycleEvents.filter(
			(ev) => ev.habitId && relevantIds.has(ev.habitId)
		).length;

		return {
			habit: h,
			parentTitle: item.parentTitle,
			isComposite: item.isComposite,
			loggedDays,
			successDays,
			goldCount,
			silverCount,
			bronzeCount,
			failedCount,
			skippedCount,
			consistencyRate,
			avgQualityScore,
			currentStreak,
			bestStreak,
			trendDelta,
			lifecycleChangesCount
		};
	});

	let selectedHabitReport: SelectedHabitAnalytics | null = null;

	if (targetHabit) {
		const todayIso = getTodayIso();
		const children = childrenByParentId.get(targetHabit.id) ?? [];
		const parent = targetHabit.parentId ? habitById.get(targetHabit.parentId) : null;
		const evolutionTimeline = buildEvolutionTimelineForHabit(
			targetHabit,
			children,
			lifecycleEvents,
			logsByDate,
			entryByDate,
			todayIso
		);

		const eventsByDate = new Map<string, HabitEvolutionItem[]>();
		for (const ev of evolutionTimeline) {
			const list = eventsByDate.get(ev.effectiveDateIso) ?? [];
			list.push(ev);
			eventsByDate.set(ev.effectiveDateIso, list);
		}

		const dailySeries: HabitDailyPoint[] = [];
		const recentValidScores: number[] = [];

		let goldCount = 0;
		let silverCount = 0;
		let bronzeCount = 0;
		let failedCount = 0;
		let skippedCount = 0;
		let totalActualMinutes = 0;
		let totalPlannedMinutes = 0;
		let cadenceLoggedDays = 0;

		for (const dIso of dateList) {
			const dt = isoToDate(dIso);
			const weekdayIndex = getPersianWeekdayIndex(dt);
			const weekdayLabel = JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS[weekdayIndex] ?? '';
			const human = formatJalaliHuman(dIso, todayIso);
			const entry = entryByDate.get(dIso);

			const evalRes = evaluateHabitDay(
				targetHabit,
				children,
				logsByDate.get(dIso),
				Boolean(entry)
			);

			if (evalRes.score !== null) {
				recentValidScores.push(evalRes.score);
			}
			const windowSlice = recentValidScores.slice(-7);
			const rollingAvgScore =
				windowSlice.length > 0
					? Math.round(windowSlice.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / windowSlice.length)
					: null;

			if (evalRes.outcome === 'gold') goldCount++;
			else if (evalRes.outcome === 'silver') silverCount++;
			else if (evalRes.outcome === 'bronze') bronzeCount++;
			else if (evalRes.outcome === 'failed') failedCount++;
			else if (evalRes.outcome === 'skipped') skippedCount++;

			if (evalRes.actualMinutes !== null) {
				totalActualMinutes += evalRes.actualMinutes;
				totalPlannedMinutes += evalRes.plannedMinutes ?? 0;
				cadenceLoggedDays++;
			}

			dailySeries.push({
				dateIso: dIso,
				jalaliSlash: isoToJalaliSlash(dIso),
				humanLabel: human.fullLabel,
				weekdayLabel,
				weekdayIndex,
				outcome: evalRes.outcome,
				tierLevel: evalRes.tierLevel,
				score: evalRes.score,
				rollingAvgScore,
				ruleSnapshot: evalRes.ruleSnapshot,
				actualMinutes: evalRes.actualMinutes,
				plannedMinutes: evalRes.plannedMinutes,
				completedIntervals: evalRes.completedIntervals,
				dayTags: entry?.tags ?? [],
				lifecycleEventsOnDay: eventsByDate.get(dIso) ?? []
			});
		}

		const loggedDaysCount = goldCount + silverCount + bronzeCount + failedCount;
		const successDaysCount = goldCount + silverCount + bronzeCount;
		const consistencyRate =
			loggedDaysCount > 0 ? Math.round((successDaysCount / loggedDaysCount) * 100) : 0;
		const goldRate =
			loggedDaysCount > 0 ? Math.round((goldCount / loggedDaysCount) * 100) : 0;

		const avgQualityScore =
			recentValidScores.length > 0
				? Math.round(
						recentValidScores.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / recentValidScores.length
					)
				: 0;

		const trendDelta = computeTrendDelta(recentValidScores, avgQualityScore);

		const { currentStreak, bestStreak } = computeStreaksFromSeries(dailySeries);

		const weekdayBuckets = Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, idx) => ({
			weekdayIndex: idx,
			weekdayLabel: JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS[idx] ?? '',
			loggedDays: 0,
			successDays: 0,
			goldDays: 0,
			scoreSum: 0
		}));

		for (const pt of dailySeries) {
			if (pt.score === null) continue;
			const b = weekdayBuckets[pt.weekdayIndex];
			if (!b) continue;
			b.loggedDays++;
			b.scoreSum += pt.score;
			if (pt.outcome === 'gold' || pt.outcome === 'silver' || pt.outcome === 'bronze') {
				b.successDays++;
			}
			if (pt.outcome === 'gold') {
				b.goldDays++;
			}
		}

		const weekdayStats: WeekdayPerformanceStat[] = weekdayBuckets.map((b) => ({
			weekdayIndex: b.weekdayIndex,
			weekdayLabel: b.weekdayLabel,
			loggedDays: b.loggedDays,
			successDays: b.successDays,
			goldDays: b.goldDays,
			successRate: b.loggedDays > 0 ? Math.round((b.successDays / b.loggedDays) * 100) : 0,
			avgScore: b.loggedDays > 0 ? Math.round(b.scoreSum / b.loggedDays) : 0
		}));

		const activeWeekdays = weekdayStats.filter((w) => w.loggedDays > 0);
		const bestWeekday =
			activeWeekdays.length > 0
				? [...activeWeekdays].sort((a, b) => b.avgScore - a.avgScore)[0]
				: null;
		const weakestWeekday =
			activeWeekdays.length > 1
				? [...activeWeekdays].sort((a, b) => a.avgScore - b.avgScore)[0]
				: null;

		const tagMap = new Map<
			string,
			{ occurrences: number; scoreSum: number; successCount: number }
		>();
		for (const pt of dailySeries) {
			if (pt.score === null) continue;
			for (const tag of pt.dayTags) {
				const cur = tagMap.get(tag) ?? { occurrences: 0, scoreSum: 0, successCount: 0 };
				cur.occurrences++;
				cur.scoreSum += pt.score;
				if (pt.score > 0) cur.successCount++;
				tagMap.set(tag, cur);
			}
		}

		const tagCorrelations: TagCorrelationStat[] = Array.from(tagMap.entries())
			.map(([tag, stat]) => {
				const avgScoreWithTag = Math.round(stat.scoreSum / stat.occurrences);
				const successRateWithTag = Math.round(
					(stat.successCount / stat.occurrences) * 100
				);
				return {
					tag,
					occurrences: stat.occurrences,
					avgScoreWithTag,
					successRateWithTag,
					impactDelta: avgScoreWithTag - avgQualityScore
				};
			})
			.sort((a, b) => Math.abs(b.impactDelta) - Math.abs(a.impactDelta));

		selectedHabitReport = {
			habit: targetHabit,
			parentTitle: parent?.title ?? null,
			isComposite: children.length > 0,
			childrenCount: children.length,
			maxSupportedTier: getMaxSupportedTier(targetHabit),
			dailySeries,
			loggedDaysCount,
			successDaysCount,
			goldCount,
			silverCount,
			bronzeCount,
			failedCount,
			skippedCount,
			consistencyRate,
			goldRate,
			avgQualityScore,
			trendDelta,
			currentStreak,
			bestStreak,
			totalActualMinutes: Number(totalActualMinutes.toFixed(1)),
			totalPlannedMinutes: Number(totalPlannedMinutes.toFixed(1)),
			avgActualMinutes:
				cadenceLoggedDays > 0
					? Number((totalActualMinutes / cadenceLoggedDays).toFixed(1))
					: 0,
			avgPlannedMinutes:
				cadenceLoggedDays > 0
					? Number((totalPlannedMinutes / cadenceLoggedDays).toFixed(1))
					: targetHabit.defaultMinutesPerInterval,
			weekdayStats,
			bestWeekday: bestWeekday ?? null,
			weakestWeekday: weakestWeekday ?? null,
			tagCorrelations,
			evolutionTimeline
		};
	}

	return {
		range,
		startDateIso: startIso,
		endDateIso: endIso,
		totalDaysInWindow: dateList.length,
		selectableHabits,
		macroStats: {
			totalLoggedDays,
			optimalDaysCount,
			partialDaysCount,
			failedDaysCount,
			overallConsistencyRate,
			totalLifecycleChanges: eventsInWindow.length
		},
		selectedHabitReport,
		comparisonRows
	};
}

import {
	buildJalaliMonthGrid,
	formatJalaliHuman,
	getDaysUntilIso,
	isoToJalaliParts,
	jalaliPartsToIso,
	shiftIsoDate
} from '~/domain/calendar';
import { parseTagsJson } from '~/domain/tags';
import type {
	AnalyticsRawLog,
	BookEntity,
	CalendarDayAggregate,
	CalendarEventEntity,
	DayHabitPerformanceItem,
	HabitEntity,
	MediaEntity,
	RawCalendarDayEntry,
	UpcomingEventBannerItem
} from '~/domain/schemas';
import {
	parseHabitSpecSnapshot,
	resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes,
	resolveHabitRuleSnapshot
} from './habit-engine';

export function computeUpcomingEventsBanner(params: {
	allEvents: CalendarEventEntity[];
	referenceDateIso: string;
	todayIso: string;
	horizonDays?: number;
}): {
	todayAndUpcoming: UpcomingEventBannerItem[];
	selectedDayEvents: CalendarEventEntity[];
} {
	const { allEvents, referenceDateIso, todayIso, horizonDays = 7 } = params;

	const selectedParts = isoToJalaliParts(referenceDateIso);
	const selectedDayEvents = allEvents.filter((ev) => {
		if (ev.isAnnual) {
			return ev.jalaliMonth === selectedParts.jm && ev.jalaliDay === selectedParts.jd;
		}
		return ev.dateIso === referenceDateIso;
	});

	const windowIsos = new Map<string, { jm: number; jd: number; diff: number }>();
	for (let offset = 0; offset <= horizonDays; offset++) {
		const iso = shiftIsoDate(todayIso, offset);
		const p = isoToJalaliParts(iso);
		windowIsos.set(iso, { jm: p.jm, jd: p.jd, diff: offset });
	}

	const upcoming: UpcomingEventBannerItem[] = [];

	for (const ev of allEvents) {
		if (!ev.isAnnual && ev.isCompleted) continue;

		if (ev.isAnnual) {
			for (const [iso, meta] of windowIsos.entries()) {
				if (meta.jm === ev.jalaliMonth && meta.jd === ev.jalaliDay) {
					const human = formatJalaliHuman(iso, todayIso);
					upcoming.push({
						id: ev.id,
						title: ev.title,
						eventType: ev.eventType,
						isAnnual: true,
						isCompleted: ev.isCompleted,
						occurrenceDateIso: iso,
						humanDateLabel: human.fullLabel,
						daysRemaining: meta.diff,
						notes: ev.notes
					});
					break;
				}
			}
		} else if (ev.dateIso) {
			const diff = getDaysUntilIso(todayIso, ev.dateIso);
			if (diff >= -1 && diff <= horizonDays) {
				const human = formatJalaliHuman(ev.dateIso, todayIso);
				upcoming.push({
					id: ev.id,
					title: ev.title,
					eventType: ev.eventType,
					isAnnual: false,
					isCompleted: ev.isCompleted,
					occurrenceDateIso: ev.dateIso,
					humanDateLabel: human.fullLabel,
					daysRemaining: diff,
					notes: ev.notes
				});
			}
		}
	}

	upcoming.sort((a, b) => a.daysRemaining - b.daysRemaining);

	return {
		todayAndUpcoming: upcoming,
		selectedDayEvents
	};
}

export function assembleJalaliMonthArchive(params: {
	jy: number;
	jm: number;
	todayIso: string;
	selectedIso?: string;
	allHabits: HabitEntity[];
	entriesInMonth: RawCalendarDayEntry[];
	logsInMonth: AnalyticsRawLog[];
	eventsInMonth: CalendarEventEntity[];
	booksInMonth: BookEntity[];
	mediaInMonth: MediaEntity[];
	upcomingEvents: UpcomingEventBannerItem[];
}) {
	const {
		jy,
		jm,
		todayIso,
		selectedIso,
		allHabits,
		entriesInMonth,
		logsInMonth,
		eventsInMonth,
		booksInMonth,
		mediaInMonth,
		upcomingEvents
	} = params;

	const grid = buildJalaliMonthGrid(jy, jm, todayIso);
	const startIso = grid.days[0]?.iso ?? todayIso;

	const habitMap = new Map(allHabits.map((h) => [h.id, h]));
	const parentHabitIds = new Set<string>();
	for (const h of allHabits) {
		if (h.parentId) parentHabitIds.add(h.parentId);
	}

	const entryByIso = new Map(entriesInMonth.map((e) => [e.dateIso, e]));
	const logsByIso = new Map<string, AnalyticsRawLog[]>();
	for (const log of logsInMonth) {
		const list = logsByIso.get(log.dateIso) ?? [];
		list.push(log);
		logsByIso.set(log.dateIso, list);
	}

	const annualEventsByJalaliKey = new Map<string, CalendarEventEntity[]>();
	const datedEventsByIso = new Map<string, CalendarEventEntity[]>();
	for (const ev of eventsInMonth) {
		if (ev.isAnnual) {
			const key = `${ev.jalaliMonth}-${ev.jalaliDay}`;
			const list = annualEventsByJalaliKey.get(key) ?? [];
			list.push(ev);
			annualEventsByJalaliKey.set(key, list);
		} else if (ev.dateIso) {
			const list = datedEventsByIso.get(ev.dateIso) ?? [];
			list.push(ev);
			datedEventsByIso.set(ev.dateIso, list);
		}
	}

	const booksActivityByIso = new Map<string, CalendarDayAggregate['booksActivity']>();
	for (const b of booksInMonth) {
		if (b.finishedAtIso) {
			const list = booksActivityByIso.get(b.finishedAtIso) ?? [];
			list.push({ book: b, activityKind: 'finished' });
			booksActivityByIso.set(b.finishedAtIso, list);
		} else if (b.startedAtIso) {
			const list = booksActivityByIso.get(b.startedAtIso) ?? [];
			list.push({ book: b, activityKind: 'started' });
			booksActivityByIso.set(b.startedAtIso, list);
		}
	}

	const mediaActivityByIso = new Map<string, CalendarDayAggregate['mediaActivity']>();
	for (const m of mediaInMonth) {
		if (m.finishedAtIso) {
			const list = mediaActivityByIso.get(m.finishedAtIso) ?? [];
			list.push({ media: m, activityKind: 'finished' });
			mediaActivityByIso.set(m.finishedAtIso, list);
		} else if (m.startedAtIso) {
			const list = mediaActivityByIso.get(m.startedAtIso) ?? [];
			list.push({ media: m, activityKind: 'started' });
			mediaActivityByIso.set(m.startedAtIso, list);
		}
	}

	const daysAggregate: CalendarDayAggregate[] = grid.days.map((cell) => {
		const rawEntry = entryByIso.get(cell.iso);
		const rawLogs = logsByIso.get(cell.iso) ?? [];
		const human = formatJalaliHuman(cell.iso, todayIso);

		const annualDayEvents = annualEventsByJalaliKey.get(`${cell.jm}-${cell.jd}`) ?? [];
		const specificDayEvents = datedEventsByIso.get(cell.iso) ?? [];
		const dayEvents =
			annualDayEvents.length > 0 && specificDayEvents.length > 0
				? [...annualDayEvents, ...specificDayEvents]
				: annualDayEvents.length > 0
					? annualDayEvents
					: specificDayEvents;

		const booksActivity = booksActivityByIso.get(cell.iso) ?? [];
		const mediaActivity = mediaActivityByIso.get(cell.iso) ?? [];

		const habitPerformances: DayHabitPerformanceItem[] = [];
		for (const log of rawLogs) {
			const h = habitMap.get(log.habitId);
			if (h && parentHabitIds.has(h.id)) continue;

			const trackingMode = h?.trackingMode ?? 'tiered';
			if (
				trackingMode === 'tiered' &&
				(log.achievedTier === null || log.achievedTier === undefined) &&
				h?.status === 'archived'
			) {
				continue;
			}

			const title = log.habitTitleSnapshot || h?.title || 'طرح حذف‌شده';
			const achievedRule =
				log.achievedRuleSnapshot ||
				(h ? resolveHabitRuleSnapshot(h, log.achievedTier) : null);

			const specSnapshot = parseHabitSpecSnapshot(log.habitSnapshotJson, h, title);

			habitPerformances.push({
				habitId: log.habitId,
				title,
				trackingMode,
				achievedTier: log.achievedTier,
				achievedRule,
				actualMinutes: resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes(log),
				completedIntervals: log.completedIntervals ?? null,
				plannedMinutes:
					log.plannedMinutesSnapshot ?? h?.defaultMinutesPerInterval ?? null,
				isArchived: h ? h.status === 'archived' : true,
				specSnapshot
			});
		}

		return {
			iso: cell.iso,
			jy: cell.jy,
			jm: cell.jm,
			jd: cell.jd,
			weekdayLabel: cell.weekdayLabel,
			fullHumanLabel: human.fullLabel,
			isToday: cell.isToday,
			entry: rawEntry
				? {
						dayStatus: rawEntry.dayStatus,
						reflectionNote: rawEntry.reflectionNote,
						journalText: rawEntry.journalText ?? null,
						tags: rawEntry.tags ?? parseTagsJson(rawEntry.tagsJson)
					}
				: null,
			habitPerformances,
			events: dayEvents,
			booksActivity,
			mediaActivity
		};
	});

	const defaultSelectedIso =
		selectedIso && daysAggregate.some((d) => d.iso === selectedIso)
			? selectedIso
			: daysAggregate.some((d) => d.isToday)
				? todayIso
				: (jalaliPartsToIso({ jy, jm, jd: 1 }) ?? startIso);

	const monthStats = {
		optimalDays: daysAggregate.filter((d) => d.entry?.dayStatus === 'optimal').length,
		partialDays: daysAggregate.filter((d) => d.entry?.dayStatus === 'partial').length,
		failedDays: daysAggregate.filter((d) => d.entry?.dayStatus === 'failed').length,
		totalEvents: eventsInMonth.length,
		finishedBooksCount: daysAggregate.reduce(
			(acc, d) => acc + d.booksActivity.filter((b) => b.activityKind === 'finished').length,
			0
		),
		finishedMediaCount: daysAggregate.reduce(
			(acc, d) => acc + d.mediaActivity.filter((m) => m.activityKind === 'finished').length,
			0
		)
	};

	return {
		year: grid.year,
		month: grid.month,
		monthName: grid.monthName,
		leadingEmptyCells: grid.leadingEmptyCells,
		selectedIso: defaultSelectedIso,
		days: daysAggregate,
		monthStats,
		upcomingEvents
	};
}

================================================================
PATH: app/core/chart-math.ts
================================================================

import type { HabitDailyPoint } from '~/domain/schemas';

export const CHART_DIMENSIONS = {
	SVG_WIDTH: 860,
	SVG_HEIGHT: 250,
	PAD_TOP: 26,
	PAD_BOTTOM: 34,
	PAD_LEFT: 36,
	PAD_RIGHT: 44,
	get PLOT_W() {
		return this.SVG_WIDTH - this.PAD_LEFT - this.PAD_RIGHT;
	},
	get PLOT_H() {
		return this.SVG_HEIGHT - this.PAD_TOP - this.PAD_BOTTOM;
	}
} as const;

export function computeMaxCadenceMinutes(
	dailySeries: ReadonlyArray<HabitDailyPoint>,
	defaultMinutes: number
): number {
	let maxVal = defaultMinutes || 15;
	for (const pt of dailySeries) {
		if ((pt.actualMinutes ?? 0) > maxVal) maxVal = pt.actualMinutes ?? 0;
		if ((pt.plannedMinutes ?? 0) > maxVal) maxVal = pt.plannedMinutes ?? 0;
	}
	return Math.max(10, Math.ceil(maxVal / 5) * 5);
}

export function getChartX(index: number, total: number): number {
	const { PAD_LEFT, PLOT_W } = CHART_DIMENSIONS;
	if (total <= 1) return PAD_LEFT + PLOT_W / 2;
	const step = PLOT_W / (total - 1);
	return PAD_LEFT + index * step;
}

export function getChartYFromScore(score: number): number {
	const { PAD_TOP, PLOT_H } = CHART_DIMENSIONS;
	const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, score));
	return PAD_TOP + PLOT_H - (clamped / 100) * PLOT_H;
}

export function getChartYFromMinutes(minutes: number, maxCadenceMinutes: number): number {
	const { PAD_TOP, PLOT_H } = CHART_DIMENSIONS;
	const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(maxCadenceMinutes, minutes));
	return PAD_TOP + PLOT_H - (clamped / maxCadenceMinutes) * PLOT_H;
}

export function buildRollingPolylinePoints(
	series: ReadonlyArray<HabitDailyPoint>,
	isCadence: boolean,
	maxCadenceMinutes: number
): string {
	const pts: string[] = [];
	for (let i = 0; i < series.length; i++) {
		const pt = series[i];
		if (!pt) continue;
		const x = getChartX(i, series.length);
		if (isCadence) {
			if (pt.plannedMinutes !== null && pt.plannedMinutes > 0) {
				pts.push(
					`${x.toFixed(1)},${getChartYFromMinutes(pt.plannedMinutes, maxCadenceMinutes).toFixed(1)}`
				);
			}
		} else if (pt.rollingAvgScore !== null) {
			pts.push(`${x.toFixed(1)},${getChartYFromScore(pt.rollingAvgScore).toFixed(1)}`);
		}
	}
	return pts.join(' ');
}

export function buildRollingAreaPath(
	series: ReadonlyArray<HabitDailyPoint>,
	isCadence: boolean,
	maxCadenceMinutes: number
): string {
	const { PAD_TOP, PLOT_H } = CHART_DIMENSIONS;
	const coords: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> = [];
	for (let i = 0; i < series.length; i++) {
		const pt = series[i];
		if (!pt) continue;
		const x = getChartX(i, series.length);
		if (!isCadence && pt.rollingAvgScore !== null) {
			coords.push({ x, y: getChartYFromScore(pt.rollingAvgScore) });
		} else if (isCadence && pt.actualMinutes !== null) {
			coords.push({ x, y: getChartYFromMinutes(pt.actualMinutes, maxCadenceMinutes) });
		}
	}
	if (coords.length < 2) return '';
	const baselineY = PAD_TOP + PLOT_H;
	const first = coords[0];
	const last = coords[coords.length - 1];
	if (!first || !last) return '';
	const lineSegments = coords.map((c) => `L ${c.x.toFixed(1)} ${c.y.toFixed(1)}`).join(' ');
	return `M ${first.x.toFixed(1)} ${baselineY} ${lineSegments} L ${last.x.toFixed(1)} ${baselineY} Z`;
}

================================================================
PATH: app/composables/useClientAuth.ts

import { ref } from 'vue';
import { getAppDatabase, type LocalAuthRecord } from '~/composables/useAppDatabase';

function generateRandomSalt(length = 16): Uint8Array {
	const salt = new Uint8Array(length);
	crypto.getRandomValues(salt);
	return salt;
}

function bytesToHex(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
	return Array.from(bytes)
		.map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
		.join('');
}

function hexToBytes(hex: string): Uint8Array {
	const bytes = new Uint8Array(hex.length / 2);
	for (let i = 0; i < hex.length; i += 2) {
		bytes[i / 2] = parseInt(hex.substring(i, i + 2), 16);
	}
	return bytes;
}

async function deriveVerifierHex(password: string, salt: Uint8Array): Promise<string> {
	const enc = new TextEncoder();
	const keyMaterial = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
		'raw',
		enc.encode(password),
		{ name: 'PBKDF2' },
		false,
		['deriveBits']
	);

	const derivedBits = await crypto.subtle.deriveBits(
		{
			name: 'PBKDF2',
			salt: salt as BufferSource,
			iterations: 100000,
			hash: 'SHA-256'
		},
		keyMaterial,
		256
	);

	return bytesToHex(new Uint8Array(derivedBits));
}

function timingSafeEqual(a: string, b: string): boolean {
	if (a.length !== b.length) return false;
	let diff = 0;
	for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
		diff |= a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i);
	}
	return diff === 0;
}

const isAuthenticated = ref(false);
const isPasswordConfigured = ref(true);
const isInitialized = ref(false);
let hasVerifiedWithServerInSession = false;

export function useClientAuth() {

	async function checkAuth(
		serverAuthenticated?: boolean,
		serverPasswordConfigured?: boolean
	): Promise<{ isAuthenticated: boolean; isPasswordConfigured: boolean }> {
		if (!import.meta.client) {
			return {
				isAuthenticated: serverAuthenticated ?? false,
				isPasswordConfigured: serverPasswordConfigured ?? true
			};
		}

		const db = getAppDatabase();
		if (!db) {
			return { isAuthenticated: false, isPasswordConfigured: true };
		}

		const record = await db.localAuth.get('auth:session');
		const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

		if (serverAuthenticated === true) {
			isAuthenticated.value = true;
			isPasswordConfigured.value = serverPasswordConfigured ?? true;
			if (record) {
				record.isAuthenticated = true;
				record.expiresAtIso = new Date(Date.now() + 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
				record.updatedAtIso = nowIso;
				await db.localAuth.put(record);
			}
			isInitialized.value = true;
			return {
				isAuthenticated: true,
				isPasswordConfigured: isPasswordConfigured.value
			};
		}

		if (serverAuthenticated === false && navigator.onLine) {
			isAuthenticated.value = false;
			isPasswordConfigured.value = serverPasswordConfigured ?? (record?.isPasswordConfigured ?? true);
			if (record) {
				await db.localAuth.delete('auth:session');
			}
			isInitialized.value = true;
			return {
				isAuthenticated: false,
				isPasswordConfigured: isPasswordConfigured.value
			};
		}

		if (record && record.isAuthenticated && record.expiresAtIso > nowIso) {
			record.expiresAtIso = new Date(Date.now() + 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
			record.updatedAtIso = nowIso;
			await db.localAuth.put(record);

			isAuthenticated.value = true;
			isPasswordConfigured.value = record.isPasswordConfigured;
			isInitialized.value = true;
			return {
				isAuthenticated: true,
				isPasswordConfigured: record.isPasswordConfigured
			};
		}

		isAuthenticated.value = false;
		isPasswordConfigured.value = record?.isPasswordConfigured ?? (serverPasswordConfigured ?? true);
		isInitialized.value = true;
		return {
			isAuthenticated: false,
			isPasswordConfigured: isPasswordConfigured.value
		};
	}

	async function saveClientCredential(password: string): Promise<void> {
		if (!import.meta.client || !password.trim()) return;

		const db = getAppDatabase();
		if (!db) return;

		const salt = generateRandomSalt(16);
		const saltHex = bytesToHex(salt);
		const verifierHex = await deriveVerifierHex(password, salt);
		const now = new Date();
		const expiresAt = new Date(now.getTime() + 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);

		const record: LocalAuthRecord = {
			key: 'auth:session',
			isAuthenticated: true,
			isPasswordConfigured: true,
			expiresAtIso: expiresAt.toISOString(),
			verifierSaltHex: saltHex,
			passwordVerifierHex: verifierHex,
			updatedAtIso: now.toISOString()
		};

		await db.localAuth.put(record);
		isAuthenticated.value = true;
		isPasswordConfigured.value = true;
		isInitialized.value = true;
		hasVerifiedWithServerInSession = true;
	}

	async function loginOffline(password: string): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> {
		if (!import.meta.client) return { success: false, error: 'محیط نامعتبر است' };

		const db = getAppDatabase();
		if (!db) return { success: false, error: 'پایگاه داده در دسترس نیست' };

		const record = await db.localAuth.get('auth:session');
		if (!record || !record.passwordVerifierHex || !record.verifierSaltHex) {
			return {
				success: false,
				error: 'اطلاعات ورود محلی در این دستگاه یافت نشد. لطفاً یک‌بار در حالت آنلاین وارد شوید.'
			};
		}

		const salt = hexToBytes(record.verifierSaltHex);
		const candidateVerifier = await deriveVerifierHex(password, salt);

		if (!timingSafeEqual(candidateVerifier, record.passwordVerifierHex)) {
			return { success: false, error: 'رمز عبور وارد شده نادرست است' };
		}

		const now = new Date();
		const expiresAt = new Date(now.getTime() + 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);

		record.isAuthenticated = true;
		record.expiresAtIso = expiresAt.toISOString();
		record.updatedAtIso = now.toISOString();

		await db.localAuth.put(record);
		isAuthenticated.value = true;
		isInitialized.value = true;

		return { success: true };
	}

	async function ensureSessionChecked(): Promise<{
		isAuthenticated: boolean;
		isPasswordConfigured: boolean;
	}> {
		if (!import.meta.client) {
			return {
				isAuthenticated: isAuthenticated.value,
				isPasswordConfigured: isPasswordConfigured.value
			};
		}

		if (isInitialized.value && (hasVerifiedWithServerInSession || !navigator.onLine)) {
			return {
				isAuthenticated: isAuthenticated.value,
				isPasswordConfigured: isPasswordConfigured.value
			};
		}

		let serverAuth: boolean | undefined;
		let serverPasswordConfigured: boolean | undefined;

		try {
			if (navigator.onLine) {
				const data = await $fetch<{
					isAuthenticated: boolean;
					isPasswordConfigured: boolean;
				}>('/api/auth/status');
				serverAuth = data.isAuthenticated;
				serverPasswordConfigured = data.isPasswordConfigured;
				hasVerifiedWithServerInSession = true;
			}
		} catch {
			// Fallback to local IndexedDB session when offline or unreachable
		}

		return checkAuth(serverAuth, serverPasswordConfigured);
	}

	async function logout(): Promise<void> {
		if (!import.meta.client) return;
		const db = getAppDatabase();
		if (db) {
			await db.localAuth.delete('auth:session');
		}
		isAuthenticated.value = false;
		hasVerifiedWithServerInSession = false;
	}

	return {
		isAuthenticated,
		isPasswordConfigured,
		isInitialized,
		checkAuth,
		ensureSessionChecked,
		saveClientCredential,
		loginOffline,
		logout
	};
}

================================================================
PATH: app/composables/useLocalRepository.ts

import { getAppDatabase, type LocalDailyEntryRecord, type LocalHabitLogRecord, type SyncMetaRecord } from '~/composables/useAppDatabase';
import { computeSmartBackupMerge } from '~/core/backup-merger';
import {
	buildCreatedHabitWithLifecycle,
	buildUpdatedHabitWithLifecycle,
	isDailyEntryEmpty,
	resolveHabitRuleSnapshot,
	serializeHabitSpecSnapshot
} from '~/core/habit-engine';
import { getTodayIso, isoToJalaliParts, isoToJalaliSlash, resolveCompletedRangeDates } from '~/domain/calendar';
import { buildDemoSeedDataset, DEMO_ENTRY_TAG, isDemoRecordId } from '~/domain/demo-seed';
import { parseTagsJson } from '~/domain/tags';
import type {
	BookEntity,
	BookStatus,
	CalendarEventEntity,
	CreateBookInput,
	CreateCalendarEventInput,
	CreateHabitInput,
	CreateMediaInput,
	DatabaseBackupPayload,
	HabitEntity,
	LifecycleEventEntity,
	LocalWorkspaceSnapshot,
	MediaEntity,
	MediaStatus,
	MutateHabitStructureInput,
	NoteEntity,
	SaveCadencePlanBatch,
	SaveDailyEntryInput,
	SyncOutboxMutation,
	UpdateBookInput,
	UpdateMediaInput,
	UpsertNoteInput
} from '~/domain/schemas';

const mutationStagedListeners = new Set<() => void>();

export function onLocalMutationStaged(listener: () => void): () => void {
	mutationStagedListeners.add(listener);
	return () => {
		mutationStagedListeners.delete(listener);
	};
}

/**
 * Optimistic local operations repository using Dexie.js.
 * Every write operation writes instantly to indexed Dexie tables
 * and stages an atomic mutation record into `syncOutbox`.
 */
export function useLocalRepository() {
	const db = useAppDatabase();

	async function stageMutation(
		mutation: SyncOutboxMutation,
		applyLocal: () => Promise<void>
	): Promise<void> {
		await db.transaction('rw', [
			db.syncOutbox,
			db.habits,
			db.dailyEntries,
			db.habitLogs,
			db.cadencePlans,
			db.books,
			db.media,
			db.notes,
			db.calendarEvents,
			db.lifecycleEvents,
			db.reflectionTags
		], async () => {
			await applyLocal();
			await db.syncOutbox.put(mutation);
		});

		for (const listener of mutationStagedListeners) {
			listener();
		}
	}

	const daily = {
		async saveDailyEntry(input: SaveDailyEntryInput): Promise<void> {
			const nowIso = input.updatedAtIso || new Date().toISOString();
			const outboxId = `daily:${input.dateIso}`;

			const isClearState = isDailyEntryEmpty(input);

			const mutation: SyncOutboxMutation = {
				id: outboxId,
				kind: 'SAVE_DAILY_ENTRY',
				queuedAtIso: nowIso,
				payload: {
					...input,
					updatedAtIso: nowIso
				}
			};

			await stageMutation(mutation, async () => {
				if (isClearState) {
					await db.dailyEntries.delete(input.dateIso);
					await db.habitLogs.where('dateIso').equals(input.dateIso).delete();
					return;
				}

				const entryRecord: LocalDailyEntryRecord = {
					dateIso: input.dateIso,
					dayStatus: input.dayStatus,
					reflectionNote: input.reflectionNote,
					journalText: input.journalText?.trim() || null,
					tags: [...input.tags],
					updatedAtIso: nowIso
				};
				await db.dailyEntries.put(entryRecord);

				const allHabits = await db.habits.toArray();
				const habitMap = new Map(allHabits.map((h) => [h.id, h]));

				for (const log of input.logs) {
					const habit = habitMap.get(log.habitId);
					const logId = `log:${input.dateIso}:${log.habitId}`;

					if (
						habit?.trackingMode === 'tiered' &&
						(log.achievedTier === null || log.achievedTier === undefined)
					) {
						await db.habitLogs.delete(logId);
						continue;
					}

					const logRecord: LocalHabitLogRecord = {
						id: logId,
						dateIso: input.dateIso,
						habitId: log.habitId,
						habitTitleSnapshot: log.habitTitleSnapshot || habit?.title || null,
						achievedTier: log.achievedTier,
						achievedRuleSnapshot:
							log.achievedRuleSnapshot ||
							(habit && log.achievedTier !== null ? resolveHabitRuleSnapshot(habit, log.achievedTier) : null),
						habitSnapshotJson: habit ? serializeHabitSpecSnapshot(habit) : null,
						completedIntervals: log.completedIntervals ?? null,
						actualMinutesPerInterval: log.actualMinutesPerInterval ?? null,
						totalActualMinutes: log.totalActualMinutes ?? null,
						plannedIntervalsSnapshot: log.plannedIntervalsSnapshot ?? null,
						plannedMinutesSnapshot: log.plannedMinutesSnapshot ?? null,
						updatedAtIso: log.updatedAtIso || nowIso
					};
					await db.habitLogs.put(logRecord);
				}
			});
		},

		async saveCadencePlans(input: SaveCadencePlanBatch): Promise<void> {
			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
			const mutation: SyncOutboxMutation = {
				id: `cadence:${input.targetDateIso}`,
				kind: 'SAVE_CADENCE_PLAN',
				queuedAtIso: nowIso,
				payload: input
			};

			await stageMutation(mutation, async () => {
				for (const item of input.items) {
					await db.cadencePlans.put({
						id: `plan:${input.targetDateIso}:${item.habitId}`,
						dateIso: input.targetDateIso,
						habitId: item.habitId,
						plannedIntervals: item.plannedIntervals,
						plannedMinutesPerInterval: item.plannedMinutesPerInterval,
						cadenceWindowMinutes: item.cadenceWindowMinutes,
						updatedAtIso: nowIso
					});

					const habit = await db.habits.get(item.habitId);
					if (
						habit &&
						(habit.defaultIntervals !== item.plannedIntervals ||
							habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval !== item.plannedMinutesPerInterval ||
							habit.cadenceWindowMinutes !== item.cadenceWindowMinutes)
					) {
						await db.habits.update(habit.id, {
							defaultIntervals: item.plannedIntervals,
							defaultMinutesPerInterval: item.plannedMinutesPerInterval,
							cadenceWindowMinutes: item.cadenceWindowMinutes,
							updatedAtIso: nowIso
						});

						const lifecycleEvent: LifecycleEventEntity = {
							id: crypto.randomUUID(),
							habitId: habit.id,
							habitTitleSnapshot: habit.title,
							eventType: 'CADENCE_UPDATED',
							effectiveDateIso: input.targetDateIso,
							summary: `تنظیم برای روز بعد: در هر ${item.cadenceWindowMinutes} دقیقه، ${item.plannedMinutesPerInterval} دقیقه (${item.plannedIntervals} بازه)`,
							reason: input.reason || 'تنظیم از شب قبل',
							previousStateJson: JSON.stringify({
								intervals: habit.defaultIntervals,
								minutes: habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval,
								window: habit.cadenceWindowMinutes
							}),
							newStateJson: JSON.stringify({
								intervals: item.plannedIntervals,
								minutes: item.plannedMinutesPerInterval,
								window: item.cadenceWindowMinutes
							}),
							createdAtIso: nowIso
						};
						await db.lifecycleEvents.put(lifecycleEvent);
						await db.syncOutbox.put({
							id: `lifecycle:${lifecycleEvent.id}`,
							kind: 'ADD_LIFECYCLE_EVENT',
							queuedAtIso: nowIso,
							payload: lifecycleEvent
						});
					}
				}
			});
		}
	};

	const habits = {
		async create(input: CreateHabitInput): Promise<HabitEntity> {
			const todayIso = getTodayIso();
			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
			const existingHabits = await db.habits.toArray();
			const maxOrder = existingHabits.reduce((max, h) => Math.max(max, h.sortOrder), 0);

			const { habit: newHabit, lifecycleEvent } = buildCreatedHabitWithLifecycle({
				input,
				maxSortOrder: maxOrder,
				todayIso,
				nowIso
			});

			await stageMutation(
				{
					id: `habit:${newHabit.id}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_HABIT',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: newHabit
				},
				async () => {
					await db.habits.put(newHabit);
					await db.lifecycleEvents.put(lifecycleEvent);
					await db.syncOutbox.put({
						id: `lifecycle:${lifecycleEvent.id}`,
						kind: 'ADD_LIFECYCLE_EVENT',
						queuedAtIso: nowIso,
						payload: lifecycleEvent
					});
				}
			);

			return newHabit;
		},

		async update(input: MutateHabitStructureInput): Promise<void> {
			const current = await db.habits.get(input.habitId);
			if (!current) throw new Error('طرح مورد نظر یافت نشد');

			const todayIso = getTodayIso();
			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
			const parent = input.parentId ? await db.habits.get(input.parentId) : undefined;

			const { updatedHabit, lifecycleEvent } = buildUpdatedHabitWithLifecycle({
				current,
				input,
				parentTitle: parent?.title ?? null,
				todayIso,
				nowIso
			});

			await stageMutation(
				{
					id: `habit:${input.habitId}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_HABIT',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: updatedHabit
				},
				async () => {
					await db.habits.put(updatedHabit);
					await db.lifecycleEvents.put(lifecycleEvent);
					await db.syncOutbox.put({
						id: `lifecycle:${lifecycleEvent.id}`,
						kind: 'ADD_LIFECYCLE_EVENT',
						queuedAtIso: nowIso,
						payload: lifecycleEvent
					});
				}
			);
		}
	};

	function buildTrackedItemMeta(params: {
		id?: string;
		title: string;
		startedJalali?: string;
		finishedJalali?: string;
		isCompleted: boolean;
		rating: number | null;
		notes?: string;
		existingCreatedAtIso?: string;
	}) {
		const { startedAtIso, finishedAtIso } = resolveCompletedRangeDates({
			startedJalali: params.startedJalali,
			finishedJalali: params.finishedJalali,
			isCompleted: params.isCompleted
		});
		const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
		return {
			nowIso,
			base: {
				id: params.id || crypto.randomUUID(),
				title: params.title.trim(),
				startedAtIso,
				finishedAtIso,
				rating: params.rating,
				notes: params.notes?.trim() || null,
				createdAtIso: params.existingCreatedAtIso || nowIso,
				updatedAtIso: nowIso
			}
		};
	}

	const books = {
		async create(input: CreateBookInput): Promise<BookEntity> {
			const { nowIso, base } = buildTrackedItemMeta({
				title: input.title,
				startedJalali: input.startedJalali,
				finishedJalali: input.finishedJalali,
				isCompleted: input.status === 'completed',
				rating: input.rating,
				notes: input.notes
			});
			const newBook: BookEntity = {
				...base,
				author: input.author?.trim() || null,
				status: input.status
			};

			await stageMutation(
				{
					id: `book:${newBook.id}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_BOOK',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: newBook
				},
				async () => {
					await db.books.put(newBook);
				}
			);

			return newBook;
		},

		async update(input: UpdateBookInput): Promise<void> {
			const existing = await db.books.get(input.bookId);
			const { nowIso, base } = buildTrackedItemMeta({
				id: input.bookId,
				title: input.title,
				startedJalali: input.startedJalali,
				finishedJalali: input.finishedJalali,
				isCompleted: input.status === 'completed',
				rating: input.rating,
				notes: input.notes,
				existingCreatedAtIso: existing?.createdAtIso
			});
			const updated: BookEntity = {
				...base,
				author: input.author?.trim() || null,
				status: input.status
			};

			await stageMutation(
				{
					id: `book:${input.bookId}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_BOOK',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: updated
				},
				async () => {
					await db.books.put(updated);
				}
			);
		},

		async updateStatus(bookId: string, status: BookStatus): Promise<void> {
			const book = await db.books.get(bookId);
			if (!book) return;

			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
			const finishedAtIso = status === 'completed' ? getTodayIso() : null;
			const updated: BookEntity = {
				...book,
				status,
				finishedAtIso,
				updatedAtIso: nowIso
			};

			await stageMutation(
				{
					id: `book:${bookId}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_BOOK',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: updated
				},
				async () => {
					await db.books.put(updated);
				}
			);
		},

		async delete(bookId: string): Promise<void> {
			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
			await stageMutation(
				{
					id: `book:${bookId}`,
					kind: 'DELETE_BOOK',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: { bookId }
				},
				async () => {
					await db.books.delete(bookId);
				}
			);
		}
	};

	const calendar = {
		async create(input: CreateCalendarEventInput): Promise<CalendarEventEntity> {
			const parts = isoToJalaliParts(input.dateIso);
			const isAnnual = Boolean(input.isAnnual);
			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

			const newEvent: CalendarEventEntity = {
				id: crypto.randomUUID(),
				title: input.title.trim(),
				eventType: input.eventType,
				isAnnual,
				dateIso: isAnnual ? null : input.dateIso,
				jalaliMonth: parts.jm,
				jalaliDay: parts.jd,
				isCompleted: false,
				notes: input.notes?.trim() || null,
				createdAtIso: nowIso,
				updatedAtIso: nowIso
			};

			await stageMutation(
				{
					id: `event:${newEvent.id}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_CALENDAR_EVENT',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: newEvent
				},
				async () => {
					await db.calendarEvents.put(newEvent);
				}
			);

			return newEvent;
		},

		async toggleComplete(eventId: string, isCompleted: boolean): Promise<void> {

					kind: 'UPSERT_CALENDAR_EVENT',
